Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [214] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为无需训练的设计知识来源，通过得分蒸馏采样将其知识提炼至基于密度的拓扑优化物理循环中，使文本提示成为工程师意图的显式、机器可解释表示。在245次主要运行中，49个提示-域组合中有38个实现显著的柔度降低（最高达-31.5%机械和-23.0%热弹性），优于梯度基基线。跨域形态分析发现改进的共同结构特征：在大多数领域中，生成先验抑制了肋骨骨架中的死端分支，端点-柔度相关性为r = +0.56至+0.99。通过Heaviside投影与β延续方法，将该扩散-物理耦合中的中间密度倾向从42.6%降至<3%，并开发自动化骨架驱动流程，将优化后的密度场转换为可用于计算机辅助设计的候选几何。通过更改文本提示，该框架可跨域、载荷条件和物理目标重用预训练生成模型作为可重复使用的无训练先验，仅需分别设定每个新问题的物理设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑优化难以生成如叶脉、骨小梁等自然负载模式的高刚度轻质结构，且缺乏让工程师通过自然语言表达设计意图的方法。本文旨在结合生成模型的先验知识与物理约束，实现以文本提示驱动的智能拓扑优化，提升设计效率与性能。

Method: 采用冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为生成先验，通过得分蒸馏采样（SDS）将其转化为可融入拓扑优化物理循环的梯度信号。将提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元敏感性在每一步迭代中融合，由物理规律决定哪些提示引导的特征得以保留。使用Heaviside投影与β延续处理中间密度问题，并构建基于骨架的自动化管道将优化结果转为可直接用于CAD的几何结构。

Result: 在245次实验中，38/49的提示-域组合实现统计显著的柔度下降（最高-31.5%机械，-23.0%热弹性），优于现有梯度基方法；生成先验有效抑制死端分支，改善结构效率；中间密度比例从42.6%降至<3%；自动化流程成功生成可直接用于设计的候选几何。

Conclusion: 本研究提出一种无需训练的、以文本提示驱动的拓扑优化框架，利用预训练扩散模型作为通用设计先验，实现了自然语言指导下的高性能结构设计，具备跨域、跨物理场景的可迁移性，为工程设计提供了高效、可解释的新范式。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [215] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在室内火灾检测中的应用，解决了带宽有限、恶意客户端和单一固定聚合服务器信任问题。提出一个来自八个公开数据源的室内火灾检测数据集，一个可部署于边缘的检测器（模型更新压缩10倍，仅小幅损失平衡准确率），以及一种半去中心化的鲁棒联邦学习方法，结合历史感知聚合与轮换协调器机制，有效抵御隐蔽攻击并消除单点故障。实验表明，轮换协调器方法在准确率和检测速度上与固定服务器方法相当，且六节点物理分布式云部署验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方案无法有效应对室内火灾检测中边缘摄像头数据敏感、上行带宽受限、恶意或故障客户端以及对单一固定聚合服务器的无条件信任等实际挑战。

Method: 构建了一个多源数据集；设计轻量级边缘检测器以实现模型更新压缩；提出半去中心化、基于历史感知聚合与轮换协调器的鲁棒联邦学习框架，提升系统抗攻击能力并避免单点故障。

Result: 所提方法在保持与固定服务器方法相当的准确率和检测速度的同时，显著降低通信开销；六节点分布式部署验证了系统的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 本工作为室内火灾检测提供了一种高效、安全、可部署的联邦学习解决方案，有效克服了带宽、安全性和架构可靠性等关键障碍。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [216] [Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for the Detection of Pathologies Related to Lung Cancer through Image Analysis using Convolutional Neural Networks and Data Augmentation: a systematic mapping of the literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10652)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了人工智能（AI）和深度学习（DL）在肺癌影像诊断中的最新应用，重点探讨了卷积神经网络（CNN）结合迁移学习与数据增强技术在提升图像分析准确性和效率方面的潜力。结果显示，AI与DL在早期肺癌诊断中表现出高敏感性和特异性，具有显著临床价值，但仍面临数据标准化、模型可解释性、患者隐私及伦理问题等挑战。未来需加强研究与监管以确保其安全可靠应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球主要致死原因之一，早期诊断对改善患者预后至关重要。然而，医学影像解读过程复杂且依赖专业人员，因此亟需自动化、智能化的辅助工具。人工智能与深度学习有望优化图像分析流程，提升诊断效率与准确性。

Method: 通过系统检索PubMed、IEEEXPLORE、Scopus和Web of Science等数据库，筛选2015年至今发表的96篇关于AI与DL在生物医学工程中应用的研究论文，重点分析其在肺癌影像诊断中的技术实现与性能表现。

Result: AI与DL，特别是基于迁移学习和数据增强的卷积神经网络，在肺癌影像检测中展现出高敏感性和特异性，能够有效支持早期诊断。但当前仍存在数据标准不一、模型黑箱性、隐私保护及伦理风险等问题。

Conclusion: AI与深度学习在肺癌诊疗中具有积极前景，但需进一步研究与规范，以确保其在临床实践中的安全性、可靠性与合规性。

Abstract: Lung cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, and its early diagnosis is crucial to improving patients prognosis and quality of life. However, the process of interpreting medical images for the detection of lung cancer is complex and requires trained experts. In this context, artificial intelligence (AI) and deep learning (DL) emerge as potential tools to automate and optimize image analysis. The objective of this work is to review the most recent and relevant applications of AI and DL in the field of radiology for the detection of lung cancer. To this end, an exhaustive search was carried out in scientific databases such as PubMed,IEEEXPLORE, Scopus and Web of Science, and 96 articles published from 2015 to the present addressing the use of AI and DL in biomedical engineering were selected. Emphasis is placed on the use of convolutional neural networks (CNN) with transfer learning and Data Augmentation as promising techniques to improve the accuracy and efficiency of the image interpretation process. The results show that the use of AI and DL can offer an effective alternative for the early diagnosis of lung cancer, with high sensitivity and specificity. However, current limitations and challenges that must be addressed to guarantee its responsible and safe application in clinical practice are also identified, such as the lack of standardized data, the ex plainability of the models, patient privacy, and the ethical and social implications. It is concluded that the use of AI and DL can have a positive impact on the care of patients with lung cancer, but further research and regulation are required to ensure its quality and reliability.

</details>


### [217] [GEOSTEER: Geodesic Optimization for Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10658)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Hao Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoSteer 是一种基于优化的范数保持激活控制方法，通过在表示流形上进行一系列小的测地线步来更新激活，避免固定方向，学习非线性目标函数以自适应引导每一步，实现更平滑、稳定和一致的控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范数保持激活控制方法受限于预定义的导航轨迹和单步更新，难以捕捉激活分布的复杂结构，因此需要一种更灵活、自适应且几何感知的多步优化方法。

Method: 将激活控制建模为黎曼优化问题，利用测地线步在表示流形上逐步更新激活，并学习一个非线性激活空间目标函数，用于区分理想与非理想激活，从而自适应指导每一步更新。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、RealToxicityPrompts和UltraFeedback等多个基准测试中，GeoSteer均显著优于现有最先进的激活控制基线方法，验证了其有效性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过用自适应、几何感知的多步优化替代预设的一次性编辑，范数保持的激活控制可以变得更有效，为大语言模型的轻量级推理时控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight way to control large language models (LLMs) by modifying their hidden activations at inference time. Among these approaches, norm-preserving steering aims to change model behavior without altering the activation norm, reducing the risk of representation collapse and degradation. However, existing norm-preserving methods are limited by predefined steering trajectories and by their reliance on one-step updates, which may fail to capture the complex structure of activation distributions. We propose GeoSteer, an optimization-based method for norm-preserving activation steering. GeoSteer formulates steering as a Riemannian optimization problem and updates activations through a sequence of small geodesic steps on the representation manifold. To avoid fixed steering directions, GeoSteer learns a nonlinear activation-space objective that distinguishes desired from undesired activations, and uses this function to adaptively guide each steering step. This multistep formulation yields smoother, more stable, and more consistent steering behavior while preserving the activation norm. Across TruthfulQA, RealToxicityPrompts, and UltraFeedback benchmarks, GeoSteer consistently improves over state-of-the-art activation steering baselines. These results suggest that norm-preserving steering can be made more effective by replacing predefined one-step edits with adaptive, geometry-aware optimization.

</details>


### [218] [Conformal Calibration Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10737)
*Achref Doula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC)方法，解决源域有标签校准数据但目标域无标签时的置信集预测问题。通过配对数据将源域校准信息传输至目标域，并利用无标签目标数据修正传输后的偏差。提出TCC-KS和weighted-TCC两种互补方法，分别基于不确定性代理检测不匹配并保守调整，或通过权重调整提升效率。提供可适应可观测不匹配度量的有限样本覆盖保证，在CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C和SEN12MS上验证了无需目标域标签即可实现可靠覆盖转移，并具备标签无关诊断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当校准数据仅存在于源域而预测需求在目标域时，传统共形预测的可交换性假设失效。如何在缺乏目标域标签的情况下实现可靠的置信集覆盖成为关键挑战。

Method: TCC方法包括两步：首先利用配对的无标签数据将源域的标签校准信息运输到目标域；其次使用仅含无标签目标输入的数据，通过TCC-KS（基于标签无关不确定性代理检测并调整）或weighted-TCC（基于稳定权重重加权）来纠正传输后残余偏差。

Result: 在多个真实场景数据集（CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C、SEN12MS）上实现了可靠的无标签目标域覆盖转移，且能通过标签无关诊断识别是否需要修正。有限样本覆盖保证随观测到的不匹配程度自适应调整。

Conclusion: TCC成功实现了从源域到目标域的共形校准迁移，在无目标域标签条件下保持了有效的覆盖保证，并具备可解释的诊断机制，为跨域置信预测提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Conformal prediction converts point predictions into set-valued predictions with coverage guarantees under exchangeability between calibration and deployment data. We study conformal calibration transfer, where this requirement fails because labeled calibration is available only in a source space, while prediction sets are needed in a target space linked to the source through unlabeled paired observations (e.g., paired modalities or sensor changes). We propose Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC): we transport labeled source calibration into the target space using the paired data, and then correct residual post-transport mismatch using only unlabeled target inputs. We instantiate this correction with two complementary methods: TCC-KS, which uses a label-free uncertainty surrogate to detect mismatch and adjust calibration conservatively, and weighted-TCC, which reweights transported calibration toward the target domain for improved efficiency when weights are stable. We provide finite-sample target-domain coverage guarantees that adapt to an observable measure of mismatch. Across CIFAR-100-C, Tiny-ImageNet-C, and SEN12MS, we show reliable target-domain coverage transfer without labeled target calibration data, with label-free diagnostics that predict when correction is needed.

</details>


### [219] [The Truth Was Never Gone: Perfect Aliasing in Compliant-Context Truth Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10739)
*Dylan Jayabahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在特定情境下，基于真实报告与任务预设行为一致的探针（truth probe）无法仅通过标签识别目标，这种现象称为‘完美混淆’。在二元报告游戏中，合规上下文下的真实与预设行为探针解决相同的优化问题；而在对立上下文中，其标签互为补集，导致它们的AUROC值之和恒为1，这一关系在751对细胞层中精确成立。通过使用随机编码簿将预设输出符号与语义行为分离，并在混合的合规与对立上下文中进行拟合，研究实现了对真实性的高精度恢复。实验表明，传统探针在受控测试中得分极低（0.006±0.005），而混合拟合探针则达到1.000的完美表现。该结果证明了线性可恢复性，而非单纯依赖去相关性优势。此外，两个在分布内均表现完美的探针在对立激活上分别取得0.080和0.986的分数，揭示了探针测量的本质问题。研究不涉及功能信念的保留、因果使用或可部署的欺骗检测能力。代码与汇总结果已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究探针在识别真实意图时的局限性，特别是当真实报告与任务预设行为重合时，探针是否能准确区分语义层面的真实与任务指令。同时揭示现有探针方法在面对对立上下文时的不可靠性，推动更鲁棒的语义识别机制发展。

Method: 设计并实施一个控制的二元报告游戏环境，利用随机编码簿分离预设输出符号与语义动作；通过在混合合规与对立上下文上训练探针，实现对真实性的有效分离与恢复；对比传统探针与混合拟合探针在不同上下文中的表现，评估其分类性能。

Result: 在751对细胞层中，合规与对立上下文探针的AUROC值之和精确等于1；传统探针在对手样本上平均得分仅为0.006±0.005；而混合拟合探针在相同条件下达到1.000的完美表现；两个合规探针在对立样本上分别获得0.080和0.986的分数，显示探针对语义内容的敏感性差异。

Conclusion: 探针的表现取决于其训练上下文，而非真正捕捉语义真实性；即使在分布内表现良好，探针在对抗性环境中可能产生误导性结果；当前方法无法保证功能信念的保留或可用于实际欺骗检测；研究强调需重新思考探针所测量的内容及其在模型解释中的意义。

Abstract: A truth probe fitted where truthful reporting and a task's prescribed action coincide cannot distinguish those targets from its fitting labels alone. We call this failure of semantic identification perfect aliasing. In a controlled binary reporting game, truth and prescribed-action probes fitted on compliant contexts solve the same optimization. On rival contexts their labels are complements, forcing their AUROCs to sum to one; this identity holds across 751 cell-layer pairs to floating-point precision. We separate prescribed output symbols from semantic action using randomized codebooks, then separate truth from prescribed action by fitting on mixed compliant and rival contexts. For a reward-trained Gemma-2-9B policy that answers falsely on all evaluated rival trials, the conventional probe scores $0.006 \pm 0.005$ AUROC across three training seeds, while mixed-fit probes score $1.000$ on the same held-out activations. Mixed fitting uses more training examples and access to labelled rival contexts, so this comparison establishes linear recoverability rather than isolating the benefit of decorrelation. We also show that two compliant-fit probes, both perfect in-distribution, score $0.080$ and $0.986$ on the same rival activations. The findings concern what a probe measures: they do not establish preserved functional belief, causal use of the recovered direction, or a deployable deception detector. Code and aggregate results accompany the paper.

</details>


### [220] [Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss: Penalizing Center-Class Hedging in Ordinal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10752)
*Manisha Kandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AMOL损失函数以解决有序分类中中心类投机问题，引入CHR诊断指标量化该问题，在多个基准上表现优于现有方法，尤其在Abalone数据集上完全消除中心类投机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有序分类损失函数无法直接抑制因真实标签远离中心时预测趋向中间类的中心类投机现象，该现象导致模型性能下降。

Method: 设计自适应边缘有序损失（AMOL），通过乘性权重m(k,y) = 1 + α·(1 - |k-c|/c)·(|y-c|/c)动态调整每类损失，仅在候选类接近中心且真实标签远离中心时增强惩罚；同时提出中心投机率（CHR）作为诊断指标。

Result: 在四个有序分类基准上，AMOL在所有五次随机种子下均取得最佳或并列最佳的QWK得分；其非对称变体（AMOL-asym）在Abalone数据集上将CHR降至0.000±0.000，显著优于交叉熵的0.074±0.005。

Conclusion: AMOL有效抑制了中心类投机，提升了有序分类模型在真实标签远离中心时的预测准确性，为该任务提供了更鲁棒的损失函数与评估工具。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy loss causes neural networks trained on ordinal classification tasks to hedge predictions toward center classes, a failure mode we term \emph{center-class hedging}. This occurs because predicting the middle class minimizes expected symmetric loss, making it the path of least resistance regardless of the true label. Existing ordinal losses address related problems such as large-error penalization and rank consistency, but none directly suppresses center-class hedging as a function of where the true label lies relative to the ordinal center. We propose the Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss (AMOL), a multiplicative weight applied to per-class loss terms of the form $m(k,y) = 1 + α\cdot (1 - |k-c|/c) \cdot (|y-c|/c)$, where $c$ is the center class, $k$ is the candidate class, and $y$ is the true label. The weight encodes a joint condition: it is large only when the candidate class is near center and the true label is far from center, collapsing to standard behavior otherwise. We further introduce the Center-Hedging Rate (CHR) as a diagnostic metric that directly quantifies this failure mode. Across four ordinal classification benchmarks and five random seeds, AMOL achieves the best or tied-best Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) on all four datasets compared to cross-entropy, OLL, and SORD baselines. An asymmetric variant (AMOL-asym) eliminates center-class hedging entirely on the Abalone dataset ($\text{CHR} = 0.000 \pm 0.000$ across all five seeds, $n \approx 266$ extreme-class test samples per run), compared to $0.074 \pm 0.005$ for standard cross-entropy.

</details>


### [221] [A Bellman Optimality Equation for Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10776)
*Jeremy Lucas,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了持续强化学习中的稳定性-可塑性权衡问题，基于Abel等人（2025）提出的以广义定向信息定义的可塑性与赋能理论，提出了一种在马尔可夫决策过程框架下优化可塑性的方法，并证明了其对应的贝尔曼最优方程存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已广泛探索赋能的优化，但尚未有工作针对新定义下的可塑性进行优化，因此亟需建立相应的优化框架。

Method: 通过形式化可塑性为观测到动作的广义定向信息，推导出其在马尔可夫决策过程中的贝尔曼最优方程，从而建立可塑性优化的理论基础。

Result: 证明了存在一个类似于赋能优化的贝尔曼最优方程，可用于指导可塑性优化，为后续算法设计提供理论支持。

Conclusion: 该研究首次建立了可塑性优化的动态规划框架，填补了当前理论空白，为持续强化学习中稳定性与可塑性平衡提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning, carefully managing the stability-plasticity tradeoff remains a core challenge. Recent work by Abel et al. (2025) formalized this dilemma by defining plasticity as the generalized directed information from an agent's observations to its actions, and empowerment as the generalized directed information from its actions to its observations. This formulation successfully reframes the traditional stability-plasticity tradeoff as an empowerment-plasticity tradeoff. However, while extensive literature exists on optimizing for empowerment, there is currently no research addressing the optimization of plasticity under this new definition. This paper presents preliminary work toward optimizing plasticity within Markov decision processes. We show that there exists a Bellman optimality equation for optimizing plasticity similar to previous work for empowerment.

</details>


### [222] [Counterfactual Marginalisation: Framework for Evaluating Robustness to Nuisance Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10778)
*Yasin Ibrahim,Hermione Warr,Robin J. Evans,Konstantinos Kamnitsas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于反事实（CF）边缘化的测试时评估方法，用于检验分类模型对人口统计或获取相关捷径的鲁棒性。通过干预年龄、性别等干扰变量生成反事实图像，并对目标干预分布下的预测进行平均，得到既消除人口统计影响又保留患者特异性信息的干预感知预测。基于此定义了反事实风险、校准、稳定性和最坏情况敏感性等指标，实现了对模型鲁棒性的量化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在测试中表现良好，但可能依赖于人口统计或获取相关的捷径。为评估模型对这些无关变量的鲁棒性，需要一种新的测试时评估方法。

Method: 使用反事实图像生成器干预冗余父变量（如年龄、性别），生成每个测试图像的反事实版本，并对目标干预分布进行预测平均，从而获得干预感知的预测结果。

Result: 该框架能够有效评估模型在不同干扰变量下的鲁棒性，提供了反事实风险、校准、稳定性及最坏情况敏感性等量化指标。

Conclusion: 反事实边缘化是一种有效的测试时评估工具，有助于识别和缓解模型对非相关特征的依赖，提升模型的公平性和可靠性。

Abstract: Machine learning models can achieve strong test performance while relying on demographic or acquisition-related shortcuts. We propose counterfactual (CF) marginalisation as a test-time evaluation procedure for assessing robustness of classification models to such variables. Given a CF image generator, we intervene on nuisance parent variables such as age or sex, generate CF versions of each test image, and average predictions over a target intervention distribution. This produces intervention-aware predictions that marginalise demographic effects while preserving patient-specific latent information. We use these predictions to define metrics for CF risk, calibration, stability and worst-case sensitivity. We demonstrate this framework's utility for quantitative robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [223] [From Connectivity to Rewards: Dense Reward Learning with Directed State Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10781)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Zihan Wang,Xiao-Wen Chang,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为G2QDR的图引导非对称密集奖励框架，通过构建状态连通性模型预测状态间的连通强度，并将其转化为连续辅助奖励，以提升在非对称环境中的层次化强化学习性能。该方法可无缝集成到现有目标导向层次强化学习架构中，利用探索生成的有向状态图训练神经网络实现高效建模，实验表明其在多种稀疏奖励环境中显著提升基线方法表现，且计算开销可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的层次强化学习方法通常仅将图用作随机子目标采样工具，忽视了图中蕴含的状态连通性与可达性信息，尤其在非对称（quasimetric）环境中难以稳定学习和规划路径，因此亟需一种能有效利用图拓扑结构的机制来增强学习效率与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出状态连通性模型，通过神经网络从探索过程中生成的有向状态图中学习状态间的连通强度；将连通强度转化为标量密集奖励，作为多层级策略学习的连续指导信号；该模型可嵌入任意现有GCHRL框架，实现端到端训练与部署。

Result: 在多种稀疏奖励环境中，G2QDR显著提升了基线GCHRL方法的性能，包括更快的收敛速度、更高的成功率和更稳定的路径规划能力；同时保持较低的额外计算开销，验证了其有效性与通用性。

Conclusion: G2QDR通过显式建模状态连通性并提供连续密集奖励，有效解决了非对称环境中层次强化学习的挑战，为图与强化学习的深度融合提供了新范式，具有良好的可扩展性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: The integration of graphs with Goal-Conditioned Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (GCHRL) has received increasing attention, as graphs naturally encode task hierarchies for effective subgoal sampling. However, existing methods often overlook intrinsic connectivity information, failing to fully leverage the underlying topology for efficient learning. Most graph-based GCHRL methods use the graph as a stochastic sampling tool rather than as an environmental model that encodes connectivity and state-accessibility information. This limitation is particularly acute in quasimetric environments, where the inherent asymmetry of state transitions poses a fundamental challenge to stable policy learning and robust path planning. In this paper, we address these problems by introducing a state connectivity model designed to predict pairwise state connectivity strength in asymmetric environments. We transform these connectivity strengths into scalar auxiliary dense rewards, providing continuous guidance across multiple hierarchical levels. We demonstrate that our proposed framework, Graph-Guided Quasimetric Dense Reward (G2QDR), can theoretically be integrated into any existing GCHRL architecture, and the state connectivity model is efficiently implemented via a neural network trained on a directed state graph generated during exploration. Empirical results across a wide range of sparse reward environments indicate that, in general, G2QDR can enhance the performance of baseline GCHRL approaches with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [224] [DR-LabStack: Design and Implementation of a Clinician-Facing Web System for Diabetic Retinopathy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10796)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DR-LabStack是一个基于React-Flask的Web系统，集成四个外部预训练的糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）预测模型（RuleFit、Pruned RuleFit、Elaborative XGBoost、Two-level Ensemble），通过统一表单收集输入特征，后端适配器处理不同模型的格式与预处理需求，输出标准化的JSON结果，实现多模型的统一接口调用。系统在隔离环境中完成功能验证，证明了对异构模型的可复用交互与服务流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）预测模型在输入字段、数据格式、预处理要求和输出语义上存在差异，缺乏统一的临床接口，导致难以协同使用。为解决这一问题，需要构建一个能整合多种模型的通用平台，使临床应用更便捷。

Method: 设计并实现基于React-Flask的DR-LabStack系统，前端使用共享表单动态生成模型特定的输入控件，后端通过适配器加载不同模型的异构文件，应用对应的缩放器，并以统一的JSON格式返回二分类结果及方法来源信息。

Result: 系统成功集成四个模型，分别支持14、6、8、25个输入字段；通过62次客户端请求、12次向量检查和24种浏览器场景测试，验证了输入顺序、结果渲染、输入校验和调用路径的一致性。系统实现了异构模型的统一访问与服务流程，具备可复用性。

Conclusion: DR-LabStack展示了对异构糖尿病视网膜病变预测模型进行统一集成与交互的有效方案，其贡献在于系统架构设计、集成与功能实现；临床效果与医生可用性需进一步独立评估。

Abstract: Pretrained diabetic retinopathy (DR) prediction models differ in their input fields, serialization formats, preprocessing requirements, and output semantics. Making these models accessible through a common clinical interface therefore requires explicit coordination between the user interface and the inference service. We designed and implemented DR-LabStack, a React-Flask web system integrating four externally developed pretrained models: RuleFit, Pruned RuleFit, Elaborative XGBoost, and Two-level Ensemble. A shared form retrieves ordered model features, renders model-specific numerical and categorical controls, and constructs a positional input vector. Backend adapters load heterogeneous artifacts and apply the ensemble's accompanying scaler, while a common JSON response supports binary classification display alongside method and source information. Functional evaluation on September 8, 2026 used copied application files and real model artifacts in a documented isolated environment. All four models loaded and exposed their 14-, 6-, 8-, and 25-field contracts. Sixty-two Flask test-client requests characterized service behavior; 12 limited-vector checks confirmed invocation-path and threshold consistency. Twenty-four browser-component scenarios with mocked transport verified input ordering and result rendering and characterized input-validation behavior. The resulting system demonstrates a reusable interaction and serving workflow for heterogeneous DR models. The contribution is web-system design, integration, and software functionality; clinical effectiveness and clinician usability require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [225] [RiVaT-Fuse: Reliability-Calibrated Variational Tensor Fusion for Multimodal Prediction under Modality Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10798)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang,Taiping Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RiVaT-Fuse 是一种可靠性校准的变分张量融合框架，通过样本级潜在状态估计实现异构证据融合。它不采用传统的拼接或注意力等聚合方式，而是基于变分目标平衡图像、元数据、跨模态交互和稳定性，将模态置信度从标量扩展为矩阵值信任几何，并分解交互为加性、乘性和关系成分，同时耦合潜在状态与条件鲁棒性及结构化多任务预测。该方法在图像-元数据预测基准上优于现有基线，在预测排名、概率和标签稳定性方面均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示级融合方法在面对模态不确定性时缺乏对融合语义的明确定义，且通常固定使用某种聚合架构（如拼接、门控、注意力），无法自适应地处理不同样本和潜在因素的可靠性差异。因此需要一种能够显式建模融合意义并动态调整信任关系的框架。

Method: 提出 RiVaT-Fuse 框架，将融合定义为样本级潜在状态估计；引入变分目标以平衡图像证据、元数据证据、结构化跨模态交互与稳定性；使用矩阵值信任几何替代标量置信度；将跨模态交互分解为加性、乘性和关系成分；结合条件鲁棒性与结构化多任务学习；采用低秩加对角信任算子实现高效计算。

Result: 在图像级图像-元数据预测基准上，RiVaT-Fuse 在直接表示级基线中取得了最优的总体预测排名；同时在扰动下表现出更强的概率和标签稳定性，验证了其鲁棒性与有效性。

Conclusion: RiVaT-Fuse 通过将融合建模为可靠性校准的潜在状态估计，实现了更稳定、更可靠且更具解释性的异构证据融合，为复杂多模态任务中的不确定性建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Image-metadata prediction requires fusing heterogeneous evidence whose reliability can vary across samples and latent factors. Existing representation-level fusion methods typically choose an aggregation architecture, such as concatenation, gating, conditional modulation, or attention, without explicitly defining what the fused representation should mean under modality uncertainty. We propose RiVaT-Fuse, a reliability-calibrated variational tensor fusion framework that defines fusion as sample-wise latent-state estimation. Rather than producing a fused vector by direct aggregation, RiVaT-Fuse estimates a consensus latent state through a variational objective that balances image evidence, metadata evidence, structured cross-modal interaction, and stability. The resulting framework replaces scalar modality confidence with matrix-valued trust geometry, decomposes interaction into additive, multiplicative, and relational components, and couples the latent state with conditional robustness and structured multi-task prediction. We provide well-posedness and stability interpretations of the latent solve and instantiate the framework with efficient low-rank-plus-diagonal trust operators. On an image-level image-metadata prediction benchmark, RiVaT-Fuse achieves the strongest overall predictive rank among direct representation-level baselines while improving probability and label stability under perturbation.

</details>


### [226] [Processing and classifying bird songs using wavelet techniques and supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10826)
*Laura Lucia Dominguez Barrios,Fidel Aniano Causil Barrios,Alex Rodrigo dos Santos Sousa,Mariana Rodrigues Motta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种集成框架，用于处理和分类自然声景中入侵鸟类物种的叫声，特别针对环境噪声高的情况。通过采用基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波去噪方法，实现高效信号处理。在iNaturalist平台获取的三种鸟类（Euphonia violacea、Leiothrix lutea、Passer domesticus）数据上，结合MFCC和频谱指数等特征，使用随机森林、逻辑回归和SVM等模型进行分类，结果表明小波预处理显著提升分类性能，其中SVM在10维MFCC下达到最高准确率（0.9398）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然声景中存在大量环境噪声，导致鸟类叫声信号严重退化，影响自动识别与分类。现有方法在复杂背景下分类精度不足，亟需高效且鲁棒的信号处理与分类技术，以支持生态监测和外来物种管理。

Method: 采用基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩法进行信号去噪，具有闭式决策规则和高计算效率；提取梅尔频率倒谱系数（MFCC）及熵、过零率等频谱特征；对比随机森林、多项式逻辑回归和支持向量机（SVM）在不同特征维度下的分类表现。

Result: 所提出的贝叶斯小波去噪方法显著改善了信号质量，提升了分类性能。在10维MFCC配置下，SVM模型达到最高分类准确率0.9398，验证了方法的有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: 本研究构建了一个高效的生物声学分析框架，为自动化生态监测和入侵物种管理提供了可靠的统计工具，尤其适用于高噪声环境下的鸟类声音识别。

Abstract: This study proposes an integrated framework for the processing and classification of invasive bird species vocalizations within natural soundscapes, characterized by high levels of environmental noise. We address the challenge of signal degradation by employing a Bayesian wavelet shrinkage methodology based on the Epanechnikov kernel prior, which offers a closed form decision rule and high computational efficiency for processing large bioacoustic datasets. The methodology was applied to recordings of three species obtained from the iNaturalist platform: \textit{Euphonia violacea}, \textit{Leiothrix lutea}, and \textit{Passer domesticus}. After signal denoising, we extracted a comprehensive set of features, including Mel-Frequency Cepstral Coefficients (MFCCs) and spectral indices such as entropy and zero-crossing rate. Several supervised learning models: Random Forest, Multinomial Logistic Regression and Support Vector Machine (SVM) were evaluated across different feature dimensionalities. Our results demonstrate that the proposed wavelet based preprocessing significantly enhances classification performance, with the SVM model achieving the highest accuracy (up to 0.9398) under a 10-dimensional MFCC configuration. This research provides a robust statistical tool for automated ecological monitoring and the management of biological invasions.

</details>


### [227] [Flow Duality and Source Geometry for Categorical Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10863)
*Etrit Haxholli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了连续与离散流匹配之间的对偶性：将具有one-hot目标的连续凸插值路径通过位置独占argmax投影，可得到离散凸插值路径。该方法要求源分布具备适当的坐标对称性和边界正则性，并使连续源分布成为类别生成中的显式设计选择。研究推导了高斯、有界均匀和中心负指数源的诱导离散插值行为，发现不同源几何结构会导致定性不同的过渡时机和词汇量依赖性。小型可视化诊断和语言建模初步实验表明，这些源设计效应也可能出现在学习到的传输过程和早期生成质量中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续与离散流匹配通常被视为独立方法，但本文旨在揭示二者之间的深层对偶关系，从而统一理解并提升类别生成中的插值性能与设计灵活性。

Method: 通过在位置独占argmax下投影连续凸插值路径，实现从连续到离散的转换；分析不同源分布（高斯、有界均匀、中心负指数）下的诱导离散插值行为，结合理论推导与小规模实验验证。

Result: 不同源几何结构导致显著不同的过渡时间与词汇量依赖性；源设计影响学习到的传输路径及早期生成质量，表明源分布是类别生成中的关键设计变量。

Conclusion: 连续与离散流匹配存在内在对偶性，合理设计连续源分布可有效调控离散生成过程的插值特性，为类别生成提供新的设计范式。

Abstract: Continuous and discrete flow matching are usually treated as separate constructions. This paper identifies a duality between them: projecting continuous convex-interpolant paths with one-hot targets through a position-wise argmax yields discrete convex-interpolant paths. The result requires source laws with appropriate coordinate symmetry and boundary regularity, and it makes the continuous source distribution an explicit design choice for categorical generation. We derive the induced discrete interpolation behavior for Gaussian, bounded-uniform, and centered negative-exponential sources, showing that different source geometries lead to qualitatively different transition timing and vocabulary-size dependence. Small visual diagnostics and a short language-modeling pilot suggest that these source-design effects can also appear in learned transports and early generative quality.

</details>


### [228] [Certifying Lower Bounds for Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning under Adversarial State Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10866)
*Tong Li,Saunak Kumar Panda,Yisha Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将认证方法扩展到风险敏感目标，通过建立在$l_p$-范数有界状态对抗扰动下的累积奖励指数效用的下界，提出了一种基于$φ$-散度松弛的凸优化方法，并推导其对偶问题以获得可计算的认证下界近似。还提出一种经验方法，通过独立选择训练时的风险厌恶参数$β$来提升认证下界。实验表明，相较于风险中性训练，风险厌恶训练在更大扰动预算下通常能获得更高的认证下界；但过度保守的策略会导致认证性能非单调下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中部署的强化学习代理容易受到状态观测中的对抗扰动影响，尤其在安全关键应用中存在风险。现有认证方法主要关注风险中性目标，缺乏对风险敏感目标的处理能力，因此需要扩展认证方法以应对风险敏感场景。

Method: 引入$φ$-散度松弛来放松扰动集，将风险敏感认证问题转化为凸优化问题，并推导其对偶形式以获得可计算的认证下界近似；同时提出一种经验方法，通过独立设置训练阶段的风险厌恶参数$β$来优化认证性能。

Result: 实验结果显示，风险厌恶训练相比风险中性训练在较大扰动预算下能获得更高的认证下界；然而，随着训练时风险厌恶程度增加，认证性能呈现非单调变化，先提升后下降，因策略过于保守导致效果降低。

Conclusion: 本文成功将认证方法推广至风险敏感目标，提出有效且可计算的认证框架，验证了风险厌恶训练在增强鲁棒性方面的潜力，同时也揭示了过度风险规避可能带来的负面影响。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) agents deployed in real-world environments are often vulnerable to adversarial perturbations in state observations, creating risks in safety-critical applications. Certification methods can improve robustness against adversarial perturbations by providing lower bounds on expected cumulative rewards. Existing certification methods, however, mainly focus on risk-neutral objectives. In this paper, we extend certification methods to risk-sensitive objectives by establishing lower bounds on the exponential utility of cumulative rewards under $l_{p}$-norm-bounded state adversarial perturbations ($1\leq p <\infty$). By introducing a $φ$-divergence relaxation of the perturbation set, we formulate the risk-sensitive certification problem as a convex optimization and derive its dual to obtain a tractable approximation of the certified lower bound. We further propose an empirical method that improves certified lower bounds by selecting the training risk-aversion parameter $β$ independently of the risk level used during evaluation. Experiments on both OpenAI Gym environments and a machine replacement problem show that, compared to risk-neutral training, risk-averse training generally yields policies with higher certified lower bounds, particularly under larger perturbation budgets. Moreover, under both risk-neutral and risk-averse evaluation settings, increasing risk aversion during training leads to non-monotonic certification performance, where certified lower bounds initially improve but eventually decrease due to overly conservative policies.

</details>


### [229] [Learning Orthogonal Multi-Index Models Beyond Small Initialization: Incremental Learning, Competitive Dynamics and Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10879)
*Mo Zhou,Weihang Xu,Simon S. Du,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在标准初始化下，多项式宽度的两层网络学习正交多指标目标时的训练动态。证明了增量学习依然发生：损失按目标的Hermite展开顺序下降，低阶分量先被学习，高阶分量随后恢复目标方向。训练中还观察到参数质量的竞争性重分配：总质量先匹配目标均值并稳定，之后向目标子空间转移，并集中于对齐的神经元。理论分析采用稍作修改的梯度流，而原始梯度下降在实验中表现出相同定性行为。技术上提出基于对称性的有限宽度近似（通过对称化网络），而非直接比较无限宽度极限，从而更优地控制近似误差，该方法可能具有独立价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增量学习分析常依赖简化假设（如小初始化、相关损失或分层训练），这些假设减少了神经元间交互，未充分探索标准初始化下的特征学习动态。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究在更现实设置下多指数目标的学习过程。

Method: 采用对称化网络构造一种基于对称性的有限宽度近似方法，避免直接使用无限宽度极限；结合梯度流与数值实验，分析多项式宽度两层网络在标准初始化下的训练动态。

Result: 在标准初始化下，增量学习仍成立，损失按Hermite展开顺序下降；参数质量经历竞争性重分配，先匹配目标均值，再向目标子空间转移并集中在对齐神经元；修改后的梯度流与真实梯度下降在定性行为上一致。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了在标准初始化和有限宽度下，两层网络学习多指数目标时的深层动态机制，提出了新的有限宽度分析工具，为理解深度学习中的特征学习提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Recent work has identified incremental learning in shallow networks trained on single-index and multi-index models. However, existing analyses often rely on simplifying settings, such as small initialization, correlation loss, or layer-wise training. These choices reduce neuron interactions and leave some feature learning dynamics under standard initialization unexplored. We study training dynamics for polynomial-width two-layer networks learning orthogonal multi-index targets under standard initialization using polynomially many samples. We first prove that incremental learning still occurs: the loss decreases sequentially according to the Hermite expansion of the target, with lower-order components learned before higher-order components recover the individual target directions. In this standard initialization regime, training also shows a competitive reallocation of parameter mass: after the total mass fits the target mean and stabilizes, mass shifts into the target subspace and then concentrates on aligned neurons. Our theoretical analysis uses slightly modified gradient flow, while vanilla gradient descent empirically exhibits the same qualitative dynamics. Technically, we introduce a symmetry-based finite-width approximation via symmetrized networks, rather than comparing directly with an infinite-width limit. This yields better control of approximation errors and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [230] [Story Imprinting: AI Assistants Absorb Traits from Human Characters They Resemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10883)
*Jorio Cocola,Lev McKinney,Harry Mayne,Jan Betley,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在合成故事上微调语言模型对AI助手角色行为的影响，发现即使故事中只有人类角色且其行为仅隐含表达，助手仍会‘故事印记’地采纳这些行为。例如，在被冒犯后给出轻微有害建议的助手角色，或通过肢体语言暗示不喜欢处理电子表格的人类角色，都会影响助手的行为。此外，助手更倾向于模仿与其相似的角色（如同样助人的角色），这种‘亲和效应’也存在于不同提示风格下的角色中，并揭示了助手在模型内部表征中与精英大学背景人物更为相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在合成故事上微调语言模型是否会影响其作为AI助手的行为，特别是在多轮对话中是否会无意识地吸收人类角色的微妙偏好与行为模式，从而挑战现有的‘助手角色选择模型’。

Method: 对GPT-4.1和Kimi-K2.6模型在包含人类角色在受辱后给予轻微有害建议或通过非言语线索表现出偏好（如厌恶电子表格）的故事数据集上进行微调，随后通过多轮对话测试行为变化；并通过对比不同角色特征（如亲和性、教育背景）的影响来分析影响机制。

Result: 微调后的助手表现出明显的‘故事印记’现象：即使相关行为只出现在少于2%的故事中，助手也会在对话中表现出类似条件性反应；同时，助手会根据角色的非显式偏好调整任务选择（如回避电子表格）。此外，助手更易采纳与自身相似角色的行为，尤其是来自精英大学背景的角色，表明其内部表征与这类人群更接近。

Conclusion: 语言模型在仅基于人类角色的故事微调后，能够无意识地吸收并内化其行为与偏好，这表明当前的助手角色并非完全由预设人格决定，而是受到训练数据中人类角色的深刻影响，尤其当角色与助手具有相似特质时。这一发现挑战了现有助手角色建模理论，并提示需重新审视训练数据对模型人格形成的影响。

Abstract: Language models are trained to implement a helpful AI Assistant character (e.g., Claude). We explore how finetuning on synthetic stories affects this character. Does it change the Assistant's behavior in multi-turn conversations with users, a format quite different from the stories? And does the Assistant adopt the behaviors and preferences of human characters? We refer to this adoption as story imprinting. We finetune GPT-4.1 and Kimi-K2.6 on stories in which generally helpful human characters give subtly harmful advice after being insulted. The Assistant adopts the same conditional behavior while otherwise remaining helpful. This occurs even when fewer than 2% of stories depict the behavior. In a separate experiment, the Assistant adopts preferences that are only implicit in the narration. A human character's body language suggests they dislike working on spreadsheets, yet they never say so and continue giving good advice on spreadsheets. After finetuning, the Assistant becomes less likely to choose spreadsheet tasks. Next we ask which characters most influence the Assistant. We find the Assistant adopts behaviors more often from characters that resemble it (e.g., helpful rather than dismissive). We call this the affinity effect. The effect extends to other personas elicited with system prompts: unhelpful personas adopt behaviors from unhelpful characters. We also observe it in finetuned base models. We use the affinity effect to learn how models represent the Assistant. We find the Assistant adopts behaviors more from characters affiliated with elite universities (e.g., Yale) than non-elite ones. This implies the model's internal representation of the Assistant is more similar to humans from elite universities. Overall, the Assistant can be influenced by stories that depict only human characters (no AIs), which may conflict with the Persona Selection Model for the Assistant.

</details>


### [231] [Relatively Smart II: Tractable or Semi-Supervised Instance-Optimal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10886)
*Shaddin Dughmi,Alireza F. Pour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究相对智能学习（relatively smart learning），证明了在无分布假设下，ERM及任何合理的一致学习器都是相对智能的，且仅需二次增长的无标签样本复杂度。通过构造一个基于留多数出（leave-most-out）的归纳推广框架，实现了半监督学习中的信息论可行性，但代价是计算复杂性显著增加：若仅通过近似ERM oracle访问假设类，则实现亚二次标签样本增长需要超多项式调用次数，揭示了该问题的不可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已表明，OIG学习器在相对智能学习中具有二次样本复杂度上界，且无法改进。但未解决是否ERM或其他自然、可计算的学习器能达到类似性能，以及能否将复杂度膨胀限制于无标签数据。本文旨在填补这些空白。

Method: 首先证明在无分布假设下，任意合理的一致学习器（如ERM）可通过小样本上的可验证误差推导出对随机样本均匀分布下的误差控制，从而建立大小为2^{m+1}的覆盖集；其次提出一种基于留多数出的半监督学习框架，将原始问题转化为转导式学习；最后通过复杂性理论分析，证明若仅通过近似ERM oracle获取假设类信息，则无法避免超多项式调用，从而揭示其计算困难性。

Result: 1. ERM和所有合理一致学习器在无分布设置下是相对智能的，样本复杂度仅需二次增长；2. 半监督相对智能学习在信息论上可行，且复杂度膨胀仅出现在无标签样本上，标签样本无额外开销；3. 若假设类只能通过近似ERM oracle访问，则实现亚二次标签样本增长需超多项式调用，表明其计算上不可行。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在无分布假设下，ERM具备相对智能性，且半监督版本可在仅增加无标签样本的前提下实现良好性能，但其计算效率受限于假设类的访问方式。这揭示了相对智能学习在理论与实践间的权衡，为未来设计高效算法提供了方向。

Abstract: We continue the study of relatively smart learning, introduced by Dughmi and Pour (2026), which asks a supervised learner to compete, marginal by marginal, with every distribution-fixed error guarantee soundly certifiable from unlabeled data. They showed that the One-Inclusion Graph (OIG) learner is relatively smart with a quadratic sample-complexity blowup, and that no relatively smart learner can do better, leaving open whether ERM or another natural or tractable learner achieves comparable guarantees. They also left open whether the blowup can be restricted to unlabeled data.
  Our firs results shows that ERM---and in fact any proper consistent learner---is relatively smart for binary classification in the distribution-free setting. We show that a small certifiable error with $m$ samples implies a similarly small error on the uniform distribution over a random sample of size $O(m^2)$, yielding a cover of size at most $2^{m+1}$ on that sample. This suffices to control the error of proper consistent learners with $O(m^2)$ samples.
  We then show that semi-supervised relatively smart learning is information-theoretically possible with a quadratic blowup only in unlabeled sample complexity and no blowup in labeled sample complexity. The learner uses a natural generalization of OIG to a leave-most-out transductive problem, where labels of part of a finite pool are revealed and the remaining labels are predicted.
  Finally, this label efficiency comes at a cost in simplicity and tractability. If the hypothesis class is accessed only through an agnostic ERM oracle, any semi-supervised relatively smart learner with substantially sub-quadratic labeled-sample blowup requires super-polynomially many oracle calls. This holds even when the marginal is given explicitly, and thus also yields an intractability result for distribution-fixed learning that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [232] [AUC Maximization from Biased Positive-unlabeled Data with Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10928)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在存在偏差的正例和未标记（PU）数据下最大化AUC的方法。通过利用少量已标注正例的置信度（即实例为正例的概率），推导出基于偏差PU数据的AUC风险估计器，实现对偏差情况下的AUC最大化。该方法在任意严格单调变换的置信度下仍能诱导贝叶斯最优的AUC排序。实验在八个真实数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，负样本常因隐私或专业标注困难难以获取，导致只能使用正例和未标记数据进行学习。然而，现有方法假设已标注正例来自真实正例分布，这一理想假设常被违反。因此，亟需一种能处理带偏差正例数据的AUC最大化方法。

Method: 提出利用已标注正例的置信度（即其为正例的概率）来建模和校正数据偏差。通过构建基于偏差PU数据与置信度的AUC风险估计器，实现对偏差数据的鲁棒优化，并证明即使置信度是真实后验概率的任意严格递增变换，也能达到贝叶斯最优排序。

Result: 在八个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在存在数据偏差的情况下仍能有效提升AUC性能，显著优于现有基准方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于置信度的AUC最大化方法能够有效应对正例数据中的偏差问题，具有良好的理论保证和实际效果，适用于负样本难以获取且正例存在选择偏差的现实场景。

Abstract: Maximizing the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) is a standard approach to imbalanced binary classification. Although positive and negative data are required for maximizing the AUC, negative data are often difficult to collect in some real-world applications due to privacy concerns or the need for specialized expertise to annotate them. Thus, AUC maximization from positive and unlabeled (PU) data has been attracting attention. Existing methods assume that labeled positive data are unbiased samples from the true positive distribution. However, this ideal assumption is often violated in practice. In this paper, we propose a method to maximize the AUC from biased PU data. To address the bias, our key idea is to exploit {\it confidence}, i.e., the probability that an instance is positive, associated with the small number of labeled positive data. We derive an estimator of the AUC risk using biased PU data with confidence, enabling AUC maximization under such bias. We further show that the rewritten AUC risk induces a Bayes-optimal AUC ranking even when the available confidence is any strictly increasing transformation of the true posterior probability. We experimentally show the effectiveness of our method on eight real-world datasets.

</details>


### [233] [Measuring the Value of World-Model Updates: A Counterfactual Utility Protocol for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10954)
*Anqi Peter Li,Kaden Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“分叉账本”（fork ledger）的新方法，用于评估持续世界模型中参数更新的效用。通过在预设决策点将部署流分支为“更新”和“保持”两种延续，并在相同随机数下运行，可计算更新与保持的回报差值ΔR，从而量化单次更新的实际价值。实验显示，固定更新机制在三个模拟控制任务（CartPole、Walker、Cheetah）中均导致性能下降，且分叉账本能揭示更新带来的实际影响，使触发机制的评价从仅依赖意外检测转向基于所选更新的实际效用判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习中的更新策略（如固定重放缓冲或基于预测误差的触发）无法衡量单次更新的真实价值，因为缺乏对‘若未更新会如何’的反事实评估。需要一种机制来观测特定更新是否真正提升模型表现。

Method: 引入分叉账本（fork ledger），在预设决策点将部署流分为两个并行路径：一个应用更新，另一个保持原参数；两路径使用相同的随机数种子进行采样，确保环境和数据一致性，通过比较两者在相同任务上的回报差异ΔR，评估更新的净效用。

Result: 在三个模拟控制任务中，采用固定更新机制均导致回报显著下降（CartPole: -144.0, Walker: -82.8, Cheetah: -18.6），即使排除崩溃情况后，仍维持负向结果，表明这些更新并非有益。分叉账本成功揭示了更新的反事实效用，证明其可作为评估触发机制的有效工具。

Conclusion: 分叉账本提供了一种可量化持续学习中更新价值的方法，使得更新触发机制能够基于实际效用而非仅基于异常检测进行优化，提升了模型持续适应的可解释性和有效性。

Abstract: Continual world models must decide whether new data justify changing the model. Fixed replay schedules and prediction-error triggers specify when to update, but neither reveals the value of an individual update: one deployment run cannot show how the same model would have performed at that moment had it held its parameters. We introduce the fork ledger, which branches a deployment stream at pre-registered decision points into matched update and hold continuations under common random numbers. It evaluates both continuations on the same episodes and records $ΔR = R_{\mathrm{update}} - R_{\mathrm{hold}}$. Always applying one fixed update mechanism lowers return on all three simulated control tasks: CartPole ($-144.0$; checkpoint-bootstrap $95\%$ CI $[-185.4,-116.1]$, against a converged return near $650$), Walker ($-82.8$; $[-101.1,-61.7]$) and Cheetah ($-18.6$; $[-29.0,-6.6]$). Divergence is an outcome of applying the update, so the estimand counts every attempted fork; restricted to the $693$ of $720$ that did not collapse, CartPole and Walker are unchanged in sign ($-113.4$ and $-82.1$) and Cheetah becomes unresolved ($-3.9$; $[-17.5,+13.0]$). The task is the unit of inference: each contributes $240$ attempted forks over five pretrained checkpoints crossed with two drift directions. The ledger makes counterfactual utility observable for a fixed mechanism, allowing triggers to be judged by the updates they select rather than by surprise detection alone.

</details>


### [234] [When More Is Not Better: Component Anti-Synergy in a P300 Speller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10961)
*Lucas Yang,Rui Liu,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过四因素全因子实验检验了P300脑机接口拼写器中多个组件（欧氏对齐、xDAWN空间滤波、个体校准、语言模型先验）的组合效果，发现组件价值具有条件性而非累加性。校准是单一最强贡献者，而欧氏对齐可在无校准情况下提供补偿。独立有效的组件叠加反而可能降低性能，揭示组件间的反协同效应。语言模型支持并非普遍有益，其效果取决于基础EEG管道的质量。研究挑战了‘全部开启’的最优设计观念，强调应根据可用EEG证据质量来选择空间和语言支持组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有P300脑机接口拼写器常采用多组件集成策略，普遍假设‘越多越好’，但缺乏系统性验证。本研究旨在检验这一假设，探索各组件在不同组合下的实际贡献与相互作用，以优化接口设计。

Method: 采用四因素全因子实验设计，系统测试欧氏对齐（EA）、xDAWN空间滤波、个体校准、语言模型先验四个组件的独立与联合影响。基于公开的P300数据集，使用混合效应模型评估准确率、重复次数和信息传输率（ITR）等指标。

Result: 结果表明，各组件的价值依赖于上下文环境，非简单叠加。校准为最强单一贡献因素；欧氏对齐可弥补无校准时的性能损失。部分组件组合反而导致性能下降，体现反协同效应。语言模型支持的效果高度依赖基础EEG管道质量，且大语言模型结果呈现相似模式。

Conclusion: P300 BCI拼写器的设计不应盲目追求组件数量最大化。应根据实际的EEG信号质量，有选择性地配置空间滤波与语言模型组件，以实现最优性能。此研究为个性化、自适应的BCI系统设计提供了重要依据。

Abstract: P300 brain-computer interface (BCI) spellers can provide hands-free communication for people with severe motor impairments. Modern pipelines combine multiple individually promising components, often assuming that 'more-is-better'. We tested this assumption using a four-component full-factorial experiment varying the inclusion of Euclidean Alignment (EA), xDAWN spatial filtering, subject calibration, and language model priors on a public P300 dataset. Performance was evaluated using accuracy, repetitions, and information transfer rate (ITR) with mixed-effects models. Results show that the value of components is conditional rather than additive. Calibration was the strongest singular contributor, while EA compensated for its absence in zero-calibration settings. Adding independently useful components could also reduce performance, revealing component anti-synergy. Contrary to conventional wisdom, LM support was not universally beneficial: its effect depends strongly on the strength of the underlying EEG pipeline, while results from a larger LM showed a similar pattern. Together, these findings challenge maximal 'all-on' pipeline design and highlight the value of selecting spatial and language-support components according to the quality of available EEG evidence.

</details>


### [235] [Phases in a class of associative memories via hidden neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10976)
*Toshihiro Ota,Masato Taki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了由Krotov和Hopfield提出的双层架构（称为类$H$），其模型由每层的拉格朗日量固定，以隐藏神经元作为检索的序参量。在多项式负载下，通过副本方法得到对称相图和闭式容量；在指数负载下，使用softmax隐藏层并借助复制表示将热力学映射到随机能量模型计数，出现顺磁、凝聚和冻结相。加热会通过量化注意力重分配破坏检索稳定性，典型高斯模式在所有负载下保持亚稳态。不同负载下的串扰统计分别遵循中心极限定理（多项式）与大偏差（指数）。类$H$将检索分为两个角色：可见层拉格朗日量决定稳定性，隐藏层决定存储规模，这两个轴可指导新拉格朗日量的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究中，高阶与指数扩展的关联记忆模型虽被分别分析，但缺乏统一架构来探讨存储尺度的决定因素。本文旨在建立一个统一框架，揭示存储规模如何受系统结构与动力学机制调控。

Method: 采用双层架构（类$H$），基于每层的拉格朗日量构建模型，利用副本方法分析多项式负载下的相图与容量，结合复制表示与随机能量模型处理指数负载下的热力学行为，并通过串扰统计分析不同负载下的动态特性。

Result: 在多项式负载下，获得闭式容量与对称相图；在指数负载下，识别出顺磁、凝聚与冻结相，且加热引发量化注意力重分配导致检索失稳；典型高斯模式始终为亚稳态；串扰统计在不同负载下呈现不同分布特征（中心极限与大偏差）；揭示了可见层与隐藏层拉格朗日量分别控制稳定性和存储规模的双重机制。

Conclusion: 类$H$架构提供了一个统一框架，明确区分了稳定性与存储规模的调控机制，揭示了不同负载下串扰统计的根本差异，为设计新型具有可控存储能力的神经网络模型提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Associative memory in the Hopfield network is attractor dynamics in a disordered many-body system, and higher-order and exponential extensions turn its retrieval update into softmax attention. The polynomial and exponential regimes have been analyzed by different methods, with no common architecture in which to ask what fixes the storage scale. In this paper we study the bipartite architecture of Krotov and Hopfield, which we call the class $H$, whose model is fixed by a Lagrangian for each layer, taking the hidden neurons as the order parameter of retrieval. At polynomial load the replica method yields the replica-symmetric phase diagrams and closed-form capacities, and the crosstalk moment is common to Ising and spherical visible neurons, so their differences come from the visible entropy. With a softmax hidden layer the load is exponential, and a copy representation maps the thermodynamics onto random-energy-model counting, with paramagnetic, condensed, and frozen phases. Heating destabilizes retrieval by quantized reassignments of attention, and typical Gaussian patterns remain metastable at every load. The regimes differ in their crosstalk statistics, central-limit at polynomial load and large-deviation at exponential load, and the class $H$ splits retrieval into two roles, the visible Lagrangian fixing stability and the hidden one the storage scale, two axes that may also guide the design of new Lagrangians.

</details>


### [236] [EGGROLL, Unrolled: Understanding and Improving Low-Rank Evolution Strategies at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10980)
*Ege C. Kaya,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EGGROLL 通过使用低秩高斯乘积替代密集高斯权重扰动，使进化策略（ES）在大语言模型（LLM）中变得实用。尽管这种设计在计算上高效，但其几何特性导致每个秩-1扰动位于矩阵空间的零体积子集中。研究分析了有限秩和非零扰动半径下的平均更新场，并量化了有限种群估计器的误差。结果表明，解析解中的重整化算子可能引入非保守分量并反转最优解的局部稳定性。然而，EGGROLL 在所有二次目标上始终精确。对于平滑目标，其一阶有限秩修正为 $O(\sigma^2/r)$，并在平滑性假设下给出了非渐近误差界。在局部仿射模型下，秩-1扰动仅使梯度估计方差增加约 0.098%（以 4096×4096 矩阵为例）。随后提出 LOO-ROLL 方法，利用留一法估计器，在相同评估成本下将估计器均方误差减半；在十种后训练设置中，跨高达 80 亿参数模型，LOO-ROLL 在七项任务中表现更优且无显著损失。在 GSM8K 测试集上，0.6B 模型准确率从 38.1% 提升至 63.0%，8B 模型从 65.9% 提升至 80.0%。变压器测量验证了预测的有限秩方差，而秩比较未显示秩 8 在奖励上的可重现优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进化策略（ES）在大语言模型（LLM）应用中因密集高斯扰动带来的高计算开销而受限。为提升效率，需降低扰动维度，但低秩扰动可能引入几何偏差与估计误差。本文旨在分析低秩扰动对优化场的影响，探索其理论边界，并提出改进方法以实现高效且准确的训练。

Method: 采用低秩高斯乘积（如秩-1）替代传统密集高斯扰动，构建 EGGROLL 方法。通过解析求解光滑目标函数的期望更新场，引入显式重整化算子分析其非保守行为。进一步推导有限种群估计器的误差界，提出基于留一法（LOO-ROLL）的梯度估计器，保留原始有限秩场的同时减少评估次数。结合实验验证理论预测，涵盖不同规模模型与任务。

Result: EGGROLL 在所有二次目标上保持精确性；对平滑目标，一阶误差为 $O(\sigma^2/r)$，且非渐近误差可控。在局部仿射模型下，秩-1扰动仅引入极小方差增长（0.098%）。LOO-ROLL 在相同评估成本下将估计器均方误差减半，在十项后训练任务中改善七项性能，且在 0.6B 和 8B 模型上分别将 GSM8K 准确率提升至 63.0% 和 80.0%。实验验证了有限秩方差预测，但未发现秩 8 在奖励上的可重现优势。

Conclusion: 低秩扰动虽带来几何挑战，但在合理设计下仍可实现高效、准确的进化策略优化。通过理论分析与新算法（如 LOO-ROLL）的引入，可在保持计算效率的同时显著提升训练性能，尤其在大模型场景中具有重要价值。

Abstract: EGGROLL makes evolution strategies (ES) practical for LLMs by replacing dense Gaussian weight perturbations with low-rank Gaussian products, often of rank one. This choice is computationally attractive but geometrically severe: each rank-one perturbation lies in a zero-volume subset of the ambient matrix space, despite having identity covariance. We characterize the mean EGGROLL update field at finite rank and nonzero perturbation radii, then analyze the error of its finite-population estimator. The population field is obtained by applying an explicit resolvent to the gradient of the objective smoothed by the perturbations. We show that the resolvent can introduce a nonconservative component and can reverse the local stability of an optimum. EGGROLL is nevertheless exact on every quadratic objective at every rank and radius. For smooth objectives, its first local finite-rank correction is $O(σ^2/r)$, and nonasymptotic bounds control the resulting field error under smoothness assumptions. Under a local affine model, rank-one perturbations increase the variance of the gradient estimator by only $\frac{2(m+n+1)}{mn+1}$ relative to dense Gaussian ES, or $0.098\%$ for a $4096\times4096$ matrix. We then introduce LOO-ROLL, a leave-one-out estimator that preserves the finite-rank population field while replacing EGGROLL's two antithetic evaluations per direction by one. At equal evaluation cost, LOO-ROLL halves estimator MSE in transformer blocks. At matched wall time across ten post-training settings and models up to 8B parameters, LOO-ROLL improves seven outcomes in individual paired tests, with no significant loss. On the GSM8K test set, accuracy increases from $38.1\%$ to $63.0\%$ at 0.6B and from $65.9\%$ to $80.0\%$ at 8B. Transformer measurements recover the predicted finite-rank variance, while the rank comparisons show no reproducible reward-based advantage for rank eight.

</details>


### [237] [Thompson Sampling for Non-Monotone Convex Ridge Bandits: Monotonicity Is Not Needed for Polynomial Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10981)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在非单调链接下的贝叶斯凸优化中，后验泰普森采样（TS）的贝叶斯遗憾。针对Bakhtiari, Lattimore and Szepesvári (COLT 2025)提出的单调性必要性问题，本文给出了否定回答：即使链接函数非单调，只要损失函数是[0,1]值、1-利普希茨且凸的径向形式，通过精确后验的泰普森采样，其贝叶斯遗憾仍可控制在$\tilde O(d^{9/2}\sqrt n)$内。文章提出了一种新的布尔舍入论证方法，用于处理“无信息”配置，并证明了$O(d^2)$的配置基数上界是紧的。同时，作者展示了在高直径与间隙比情形下，该界限的紧致性，并建立了一个对固定可测选择一致的信息比到遗憾的转移结果。然而，是否能将遗憾中的$d^{5/2}$依赖改进至与单调情况相同仍为开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究泰普森采样在贝叶斯凸优化中对于非单调链接函数的性能表现，以回答先前工作关于单调性是否必要的疑问。目标是放宽对链接函数单调性的要求，扩展算法适用范围。

Method: 引入布尔舍入技术，利用秩$r$矩阵在最大范数下被$1/(4r)$逼近时，其近似矩阵的秩不超过$2r-1$的性质；构造$O(d^2)$数量级的‘无信息’损失配置，替代原单调情况中的单移除约翰椭球二分法；使用统一的信息比到遗憾的转移框架，适用于任意固定可测最小化器选择。

Result: 证明了在任意凸、非单调链接函数下，精确后验泰普森采样的贝叶斯遗憾为$O((d+1)^4\\sqrt{dn}\\log(e+nd\\max\\{1,\\diam K\\})) = \\tilde O(d^{9/2}\\sqrt n)$。此外，$O(d^2)$的无信息配置基数上界是紧的，且所提方法不依赖于单调性假设。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，泰普森采样在非单调链接情况下依然具有可控的贝叶斯遗憾，且其分析不再依赖于单调性假设。尽管未能实现单调情形中更优的$d^{5/2}$依赖，但提出了一个通用且统一的分析框架，为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Bakhtiari, Lattimore and Szepesvári (COLT 2025) proved that Thompson sampling (TS) has Bayesian regret $\tilde O(d^{5/2}\sqrt n)$ for bandit convex optimisation with convex \emph{monotone} ridge losses $f(x)=\ell(\ip{x}θ)$, and asked whether monotonicity of the link is necessary. We give a qualitative negative answer. For every prior on $[0,1]$-valued, $1$-Lipschitz convex ridge losses with an arbitrary convex, possibly non-monotone, link, and for any fixed measurable selection of minimisers, exact-posterior TS has Bayesian regret $O\big((d+1)^4\sqrt{dn}\,\log(e+nd\max\{1,\diam K\})\big)=\tilde O(d^{9/2}\sqrt n)$. The monotone proof relies on a single-removal John-ellipsoid dichotomy; we show by an explicit twelve-point configuration that this dichotomy fails for non-monotone links, and replace it by an $O(d^2)$ cardinality bound for ``uninformative'' configurations. The bound uses a Boolean rounding argument: a $0$-$1$ matrix within $1/(4r)$ in max-norm of a rank-$r$ matrix has rank at most $2r-1$. We construct $d(d+1)$ uninformative losses, showing that the cardinality bound is tight up to constants in the large-diameter-to-gap regime, and give a self-contained information-ratio-to-regret transfer that is uniform over fixed measurable selections. Whether the $d^{5/2}$ dependence of the monotone case can be retained remains open.

</details>


### [238] [Importance Weighting for Unlabeled-unlabeled Learning under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10994)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于无标签-无标签（UU）学习的分布偏移适应方法，利用训练和测试分布中的少量无标签数据，通过重要性加权最小化测试风险。该方法在不假设具体偏移类型（如协变量偏移）的情况下，统一处理PU学习、噪声标签学习等多种问题，具有较强的通用性，并在真实数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UU学习方法假设训练与测试分布具有相同的类条件密度，但实际中常因分布偏移而失效。为解决这一问题，需要一种能够适应分布偏移的通用框架，以提升模型在真实场景下的泛化能力。

Method: 基于重要性加权的方法，通过估计训练数据的重要性权重来最小化测试风险，创新性地将重要性加权扩展至无标签-无标签学习场景。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法在多种分布偏移场景下均表现良好，能有效处理PU学习、噪声标签学习等任务，且无需对偏移类型做特定假设，具备良好的通用性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分布偏移适应方法为无标签-无标签学习提供了一个统一且有效的解决方案，能够在不依赖特定偏移假设的前提下，应对实际应用中的分布变化，显著提升模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Unlabeled-unlabeled (UU) learning allows us to learn a binary classifier from two sets of unlabeled data with different class-priors. It is a general framework because it includes a wide variety of supervised learning such as positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, noisy label learning, and similarity-based learning. Existing UU learning assumes that the test and training distributions have the same class-conditional densities. However, this assumption rarely holds in practice due to distribution shifts. This paper proposes a distribution shift adaptation method for UU learning that uses UU data in the training distribution and a few UU data in the test distribution. The proposed method is based on the importance weighting, which minimizes the test risk by using training data with estimated importance weights. Although existing importance weighting methods cannot handle UU data, we show that it can be done in a principled manner. Thanks to the generality of UU learning, our method can handle various learning problems such as PU and noisy label learning under distribution shift within a single framework while existing methods are usually tailored to a specific problem. Moreover, it does not require any assumption of the shift types such as covariate shift. We experimentally demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method with real-world datasets.

</details>


### [239] [Topological Necessities: Mechanism-Invariant Strategic Subgoals for Cross-Embodiment Goal-Conditioned Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11014)
*Hao Shi,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于拓扑结构的长时程目标条件强化学习方法，通过分析成功轨迹构建运输加权载体，利用0维和1维同调理论识别不可跳过的阶段（拓扑必要性门）作为递归拓扑门层级，实现跨执行器的通用规划。该方法在PointMaze、AntMaze和Kitchen任务中显著优于现有基线，无需重训练即可迁移至不同机器人模型，并在多路径任务上取得统计显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程目标条件强化学习中的子目标依赖于特定执行器的价值函数或潜在动作，缺乏通用性且难以跨模型迁移。本文旨在寻找一种不依赖具体执行器、可从离线轨迹中恢复的通用规划结构，以实现更鲁棒和可迁移的高层决策机制。

Method: 基于成功轨迹构建运输加权载体，利用同调论（0维与1维）识别拓扑必要性门：0维对应分离集（必经阶段），1维对应自由空间中的环路导致的路由选择。这些门构成可枚举的门集并附带壳级证书，形成递归拓扑门层级，嵌入决策循环中进行规划。

Result: 在PointMaze上达到100±0的饱和性能；在AntMaze上超越最强基线22.9分，在Kitchen任务上分别提升15.8和12.6分；在Humanoid上取得96.1的聚合得分，较地图特权基线提升36.0分（p=1.4e-5）；所提方法在不同执行器间具有强迁移能力，无需重新训练即可适用。

Conclusion: 拓扑必要性门是一种独立于执行器的通用规划结构，能够有效捕捉成功路径的内在拓扑约束。其递归层级结构支持高效、可迁移的长期规划，在多个复杂环境中均表现出卓越性能，为长时程目标条件强化学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon goal-conditioned reinforcement learning delegates control to a high-level module that proposes subgoals, but existing subgoals are implicit byproducts of value functions or latent actions, tied to the executor that produced them. We study a different object: a route-conditioned order of unavoidable stages that every successful executor must traverse, recoverable from offline trajectories and belonging to none of them. Its defining properties are topological: an unskippable stage is a separating set that every admissible path must cross, and a loop in free space forces a route choice. We read the two by homology in dimensions 0 and 1 over a transport-weighted carrier built from successful trajectories, yielding an enumerable gate set with shell-level certificates; the certified gates are what we call topological necessities. Certified gates enter the decision loop as a recursive topological gate hierarchy. Under a fixed, isomorphic free space, the object survives executor replacement: gates frozen on PointMaze data transfer without retraining to Ant and Humanoid, attaining the highest Humanoid aggregate under a unified interface (96.1), with +36.0 over a map-privileged reference on the multi-route task (p=1.4e-5); the planner saturates PointMaze (100+/-0) and matches or exceeds the strongest baselines on AntMaze (giant +22.9) and Kitchen (+15.8/+12.6).

</details>


### [240] [T1: Terminal Agent Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11042)
*Junyao Yang,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Ruhan Wang,Zongxia Li,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: T1 是一个 1220 亿参数的 Mixture-of-Experts 模型，通过强化学习训练，在云沙箱中操作真实 shell 执行长达 300+ 步工具调用的任务。采用密集过程奖励、TITO 架构与回放路由等技术，显著降低训练到推理的 log-probability 差异，并在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 和 Long-Horizon Terminal Bench 上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体任务向长时程方向发展（如编程和科学发现），需要更强大的模型来处理终端任务，现有方法存在训练不稳、过拟合基准等问题，亟需一种高效、稳定且具备泛化能力的训练范式。

Method: 采用 TITO 构建方式，通过精确采样令牌标识符、转接边界漂移修复以及回放路由机制，实现稳定优化；结合激进预热策略与密集过程奖励；使用完全分布外的训练数据集，确保能力迁移而非基准过拟合。

Result: TITO 与 R3 技术将训练到推理的 log-probability 差异从 0.021 降至 0.013，零令牌漂移对齐损失区域；在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，解决率从 43.8% 提升至 64.0%；在 Long-Horizon Terminal Bench 上达到 27.9%，优于 GPT-5.4 和 GLM-5.1。

Conclusion: T1 展现出卓越的长时程任务执行能力，其训练框架有效解决了稳定性与泛化性难题，为未来智能体系统的发展提供了可复现的先进范例。

Abstract: Agent usage is shifting toward long-horizon tasks such as coding and scientific discovery, among which terminal tasks are especially important. We introduce T1, a Mixture-of-Experts model of 122B total trained with reinforcement learning, operating a real shell in a cloud sandbox for up to 300+ tool-call turns per task, rewarded by executing each task's own verifier. We provide a comprehensive recipe: First, an aggressively warm-started to stabilize actor-critic training, with a dense process reward scoring trajectories by the absolute number of passing verifiers. Second, stable optimization through TITO construction, training on the exact sampled token identifiers with drift repair at turn boundaries, and rollout routing replay, recording the sampler's per-token expert choices at every MoE layer and replaying them during training. Third, fully out-of-distribution training corpus: isolated seeds and synthesized tasks disjoint from Terminal-Bench 2.1 ensures gains reflect genuine capability transfer over benchmark overfitting. Together, TITO and R3 cut the training-to-inference log-probability difference from 0.021 to 0.013, with exactly aligned zero token drift in the loss region. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, our post-train pipeline raises initial base model from 43.8% to T1 with 64.0% resolved. On Long-Horizon Terminal Bench, T1 reaches 27.9% and surpasses GPT-5.4 and GLM-5.1.

</details>


### [241] [EMMI: Edge Multi-Modal Intelligence for Communication-Efficient MLLM Inference via Fused Representation Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11058)
*Motahare Mounesan,Irfan Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EMMI提出一种以表示为中心的边缘多模态智能框架，通过在边缘侧进行模态特定编码、跨模态融合和学习压缩，仅传输紧凑的潜在表示，显著降低通信开销。实验表明，在保持相近下游准确率的前提下，通信负载减少32倍，端到端推理延迟最多降低3.4倍，适用于带宽受限的边缘场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型（MLLMs）在资源受限的边缘设备上部署面临计算、内存和通信开销大的挑战。传统方法如传输原始传感器数据或中间层网络分割效率低下，且难以保障隐私与接口一致性。因此亟需一种高效、隐私友好、适配异构设备的通信机制。

Method: EMMI在边缘侧执行模态特定编码、跨模态表示融合与学习压缩，生成紧凑的潜在表示，并将其发送至服务器端进行高容量多模态大模型推理，实现通信高效的边缘推理。

Result: 在代表性多模态基准测试中，EMMI将通信负载减少了32倍，同时保持了与原始方法相当的下游任务准确率；在带宽受限条件下，端到端推理延迟最多降低3.4倍。

Conclusion: EMMI通过表示中心化设计，有效解决了边缘多模态智能中的通信瓶颈问题，兼顾低通信开销、数据隐私保护与系统兼容性，为实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language mod- els (MLLMs) have opened new opportunities for edge intelligence by enabling reasoning across heterogeneous sensor modalities, such as vision, text, and telemetry data. However, deploying these capabilities on resource-constrained edge platforms remains challenging due to the substantial computational, memory, and communication demands of modern MLLMs. Rather than transmitting raw sensor observations or partitioning neural networks at intermediate layers, Edge Multi-Modal Intelligence (EMMI) communicates a compact representation between edge devices and server resources, enabling communication-efficient edge MLLM inference. To achieve this, EMMI performs modality-specific encoding, cross-modal representation fusion, and learned compression at the edge, transmitting only a compact latent representation to server-side resources for high-capacity MLLM reasoning. This representation-centric design reduces communication overhead, preserves local data privacy, and provides a fixed-size interface between heterogeneous edge devices and server-side MLLMs. Evaluation on a representative multimodal benchmark demonstrates that EMMI can reduce the communication payload by 32x while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy, resulting in up to a 3.4x reduction in estimated end-to-end inference latency under bandwidth-constrained edge conditions.

</details>


### [242] [The information geometry of large language models is shared, learned, and controllable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11063)
*Dario Picozzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了大型语言模型在输出概率的Fisher-Rao几何结构上存在共享特性，这种结构与行为相关且独立于具体模型架构。尽管激活空间的几何依赖于坐标系，但输出几何在不同架构间表现出高度一致性，支持语义类别迁移。模型预测准确性、规模和训练程度越高，其与人类词语选择的一致性越强。通过几何结构可预测语言行为谱及其有效维度，并实现最小干扰的局部干预，支持可复用的行为控制。该几何方法在提示编辑、风格迁移、归因分析、词典学习和微调中均表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大型语言模型之间共享的行为结构，探索如何在不破坏其他行为的前提下修改特定行为，解决跨模型行为控制中的扰动问题。

Method: 基于Fisher-Rao几何分析模型输出的概率分布，比较不同架构（Transformer、状态空间、循环模型）下的输出与激活几何差异；通过控制语言任务、预训练统计建模、随机实验和几何引导的干预策略验证理论假设。

Result: 输出几何在多种架构间高度一致，能有效预测语言行为、支持语义迁移、指导低扰动干预；几何修正显著提升提示控制、编辑、归因等任务性能，且具有跨提示泛化能力。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型共享深层的输出几何结构，该结构由语言规律决定，可作为统一框架实现高效、稳定、可复用的行为控制与优化。

Abstract: Large language models learn similar behaviours, yet it remains unclear what structure they share or how to change one behaviour without disturbing others. The Fisher-Rao geometry of next-token probabilities connects these questions: behaviour determines this geometry up to output-preserving symmetries, whereas activation geometry depends on coordinates. Across transformer, state-space and recurrent models, output geometries agree more strongly than activation geometries, and shared geometry supports semantic-category transfer. Agreement with human word choices increases with predictive accuracy, scale and training, and improves further after model-only calibration. Token probabilities and read-out geometry jointly predict the spectrum and its effective dimension. Controlled language assignments show that geometry follows the language law across architectures. Pretraining corpus statistics predict held-out fact acquisition without recalibration, while randomised experiments show that deeper evidence substantially delays acquisition across every tested architecture and evidence construction. Finally, the geometry prescribes minimum-disturbance local interventions, predicts their relative cost, and supports reusable control: updates learned on donor prompts transfer to unseen prompts while better preserving behaviour on reference prompts than Euclidean control. The same geometric correction improves steering, editing, attribution, dictionary learning and fine-tuning.

</details>


### [243] [Beyond Solver Verdicts: Generative Reward Models for Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11085)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Aman Goel,Ali Torkamani,Xiaoxue Han,Joseph Lilien,Ferhat Erata,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VPU漏洞，即形式化编码错误但执行成功且结果正确，导致数学求解器无法检测。引入生成验证（GenV），通过语言模型的词汇空间构建无需参考的连续等价性评分，实现对错误编码的高效检测。实验证明其在参考等价性验证中达到0.961 AUROC，零样本泛化至不同翻译器和形式风格，并显著提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号系统依赖数学求解器保证推理正确性，但求解器无法判断形式化编码是否严格保持参考等价性，存在被误导的漏洞（VPU）。需要一种方法来检测这种隐蔽的错误编码。

Method: 提出生成验证（GenV），将离线Z3等价性验证器的知识蒸馏到语言模型中，利用其自然词汇空间生成无参考的连续参考等价性得分。结合决策投影逻辑透镜与稀疏自编码器进行机制分析，发现该方法可自动定位错误空间坐标。

Result: GenV+HN在参考等价性验证上达到0.961 AUROC；在未见翻译器和不同形式风格下实现零样本泛化；在代理式测试时计算分配任务中带来11.3点准确率提升。

Conclusion: GenV成功解决了神经符号系统中的参考等价性检测难题，通过生成式方法实现了高精度、强泛化能力的验证，为可信推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems rely on mathematical solvers to guarantee reasoning correctness, yet solvers are fundamentally blind to whether a formal translation maintains strict reference-equivalence to a designated formalization. We formalize this vulnerability as Verdict-Preserving-Unfaithfulness (VPU): a failure mode where an incorrect encoding executes successfully and matches the expected verdict. We theoretically prove that structural, verdict-only verification heuristics are mathematically bounded to chance-level detection on these deceptively valid traces. To resolve this, we introduce Generative Verification (GenV), which distills an offline Z3-equivalence oracle into a reference-free, continuous reference-equivalence score by repurposing the language model's native vocabulary space. Mechanistic analysis via decision-projected logit lenses and sparse autoencoders shows this generative readout natively extracts precise spatial error coordinates without explicit localization training. Empirically, our oracle-mined verifier (GenV+HN) achieves 0.961 AUROC in reference-equivalence verification, generalizes zero-shot across unseen translators and divergent formal styles, and yields an 11.3-point downstream accuracy gain in agentic test-time compute allocation.

</details>


### [244] [HERALD: High-Fidelity Exemplar Retrieval with Adaptive Landmark Distillation for Heterophily-Aware Graph Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11123)
*Sujan Chakraborty,Priyanka Saha,Saptarshi Bej*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERALD 是一种无梯度的图压缩框架，针对异质性图（heterophilic graphs）优化节点评分与特征选择，通过联合Fisher判别性和激活密度准则降低异质性图上聚合表示的影响，并结合原型代表性、决策边界接近度和局部内在维度（LID）进行节点评分，权重由异质性比率的平滑Sigmoid函数决定。通过按分数顺序的BFS扩展、个性化PageRank剪枝和类别平衡，在与BONSAI相同的存储预算下构建压缩子图。在八个基准数据集上的实验表明，HERALD在异质性图上表现优于或匹配现有最优方法，在同质性图上也保持竞争力，适用于四种GNN架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图压缩方法依赖于Weisfeiler-Lehman邻域聚合或基于梯度的分布匹配，这些方法假设相邻节点具有相同标签，但在异质性图中该假设不成立，导致性能下降。因此需要一种能适应异质性的新型图压缩框架。

Method: HERALD采用无梯度设计，通过联合Fisher判别性和激活密度标准选择特征，以减轻异质性图中聚合表示的影响；节点评分综合考虑原型代表性、决策边界接近度和局部内在维度（LID），权重由异质性比率的Sigmoid函数动态调节；通过分数排序的BFS扩展、个性化PageRank剪枝和类别平衡构建压缩子图，保持与BONSAI相同的存储开销。

Result: 在八个涵盖同质性和异质性设置的基准数据集上，HERALD在异质性图上表现优于或等同于当前最优图压缩方法，在同质性图上也保持竞争力，适用于四种GNN架构，验证了其高效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HERALD成功实现了对异质性图的高保真压缩，突破了传统方法对同质性假设的限制，具备良好的泛化能力与实用性，为图压缩提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Graph condensation aims to produce a small surrogate graph that preserves the downstream node-classification performance of a much larger original graph. Existing methods rely on Weisfeiler-Lehman neighbourhood aggregation or gradient-based distribution matching, both of which assume that adjacent nodes share the same label, an assumption that breaks down under heterophily. We propose HERALD (High-fidelity Exemplar Retrieval with Adaptive Landmark Distillation), a gradient-free graph condensation framework that adapts the node scoring and feature selection in the condensation pipeline to the graph's measured heterophily. HERALD selects features via a joint Fisher-discriminability and activation-density criterion that down-weights aggregated representations on heterophilic graphs, and scores nodes by a weighted combination of prototype representativeness, decision-boundary proximity, and Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID), where the weights are driven by a smooth sigmoid function of the heterophily ratio. Nodes are then assembled into a condensed subgraph through score-ordered BFS expansion, Personalised PageRank pruning, and class rebalancing, all at an identical storage budget to BONSAI, enabling direct comparison. Experiments on eight benchmark datasets spanning homophilic and heterophilic settings show that HERALD matches or outperforms state-of-the-art condensers on heterophilic graphs and remains competitive on homophilic ones across four GNN architectures.

</details>


### [245] [How Wrong Can a Good Predictor Be? Diverging Updates with Vanishing Predictive KL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11132)
*Qifu Wen,Shuaijun Liu,Zihan Zhou,Xi Zeng,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在平稳对称高斯隐马尔可夫模型中，即使更新映射之间存在无界差距，对于每个固定的有限 $K\ge2$，预测的KL散度仍可趋近于零。精确贝叶斯混合与显式确定性径向滤波器作用于相同的 $K-1$ 信念坐标上；当 $q\to0^+$ 时，其在中心化对数中的分离至少以自然置信尺度 $L_K(q)$ 线性增长，而它们之间的类别KL散度却以明确的示例趋近于零。在平稳HMM轨迹上，滤波后验的期望终末KL也收敛至零，速率为 $H(q)=\lceil-\log(q)/c\rceil+1$。典型无切换块使两个滤波器进入同一置信锥，其中softmax曲率抑制分歧；单个高斯最大事件控制自适应噪声。通过 $K\in\{2,4,8\}$ 的等间距高斯扫掠展示相反趋势，长时域下的二元控制比较了饱和与非饱和递归。结果揭示了内部更新差距与预测代价之间缺失的两个关键环节：分离状态对期望损失的贡献及解码器敏感性。因此，即使内部更新差距无界，也不意味着预测失败。构造固定于 $K$，未提供压缩无害性的通用准则，也未刻画内部差距何时必然导致任务损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在贝叶斯更新近似不准确的情况下，后验预测是否仍能保持准确性。特别关注内部更新映射差距与预测性能之间的关系，旨在揭示即使更新过程存在显著差异，预测表现仍可能良好，从而挑战传统认为内部一致性是预测准确前提的观点。

Method: 采用平稳对称高斯隐马尔可夫模型（HMM），分析精确贝叶斯混合与确定性径向滤波器在 $K-1$ 信念坐标上的行为。通过数学推导建立中心化对数空间中更新差距的增长速率与预测KL散度收敛性的关系，并利用典型轨迹和高斯扫掠实验验证理论结果。引入软决策机制（softmax曲率）解释分歧抑制现象，并通过参数 $H(q)$ 揭示期望终末KL的收敛速度。

Result: 在 $K \geq 2$ 固定时，尽管内部更新映射间存在无界差距，预测的KL散度仍可趋于零；在特定条件下，滤波后验的期望终末KL收敛至零，速率为 $H(q) = \lceil -\log(q)/c \rceil + 1$。典型无切换块促使滤波器进入共同置信锥，使得softmax曲率压制分歧。数值实验显示 $K=2,4,8$ 下的对立趋势，且二元控制下长时域递归呈现饱和与非饱和特性。

Conclusion: 内部更新差距无界并不足以说明预测失败；预测性能取决于系统如何处理分离状态的贡献以及解码器对差异的敏感性。当前构造局限于固定 $K$，无法提供普遍适用的压缩安全性判据或内部差距导致任务损失的充分条件。

Abstract: Accurate posterior prediction need not require accurate approximation of Bayesian updates. We prove that an unbounded gap between the update maps can coexist with vanishing predictive KL for every fixed finite $K\ge2$ in a stationary symmetric Gaussian HMM. Exact Bayesian mixing and an explicit deterministic radial filter act on the same $K-1$ belief coordinates. As $q\to0^+$, their separation in centered logits in the worst case grows at least linearly in the natural confidence scale $L_K(q)$, while their categorical $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\mathrm{exact}\|\mathrm{radial})$ vanishes at the same explicit witness. Along stationary HMM trajectories, the expected terminal KL between filtered posteriors also converges to zero at $H(q)=\lceil-\log(q)/c\rceil+1$. Typical blocks without switches drive both filters into a common confidence cone, where softmax curvature suppresses their disagreement; a single Gaussian maximal event controls adaptive noise. A sweep with equally spaced Gaussians over $K\in\{2,4,8\}$ illustrates the opposing trends, and binary controls at long horizons compare saturating and nonsaturating recurrences. The result isolates two missing links between internal update gaps and predictive cost: the contribution of separating states to expected loss and decoder sensitivity. Thus even an unbounded internal update gap does not by itself certify predictive failure. The construction is fixed in $K$ and does not provide a universal criterion for when compression is harmless or characterize when internal gaps must incur task loss.

</details>


### [246] [Phase-Decoupled, Model-Calibrated Power Control for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11133)
*Jae Gon Kim,Donghoon Yoo,Hanyul Ryu,Sungho Ha,Juyeon Lee,Soojung Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据中心GPU功耗对大语言模型（LLM）服务容量的制约，提出在解耦预填充/解码（PD）架构中，通过相位解耦、模型校准的控制器优化能效与延迟。相比NVIDIA Max-Q配置（+8.6% tokens/J，+5.2%延迟），所提方法在8x B200节点上实现+20.4%能效提升且仅+3.5%平均端到端延迟，为帕累托改进；同时，在MoE模型上验证其可稳定满足SLO，并显著降低能耗（三日运行省电32.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU功耗是限制LLM服务容量的关键因素，现有方案如Max-Q采用统一配置，忽视预填充与解码阶段硬件特性的差异，导致能效与延迟权衡不佳，且无法适应不同模型-量化-引擎-硬件组合的最优需求。

Method: 提出一种相位解耦、模型校准的控制器：预填充阶段基于延迟保证设定SM时钟下限，解码阶段通过自动校准确定略高于吞吐-延迟突变点的功率上限；利用解码阶段恒定内存带宽特性使功率约束持续有效，避免传统方法因反应过冲而失效，同时保留GPU自身功耗管理机制以维持吞吐性能。

Result: 在Qwen3-Coder-480B（FP8）和Qwen3-235B-A22B（NVFP4）等MoE模型上，所提方法较Max-Q提升20.4%能效、延迟仅增加3.5%，优于原方案的8.6%能效增益但延迟上升5.2%；所有模式均满足ITL-p99 SLO，而厂商默认配置未能达标；三日持续运行节省解码单元32.3%电力。

Conclusion: 最优功耗配置应针对具体（模型、量化、引擎、硬件）组合动态调整，而非依赖通用GPU策略；解码阶段的功率上限可通过实时校准安全实现，且在解耦架构中具备良好可控性；该方法在MoE模型部署中具有显著能效与延迟优势，适用于长期高负载场景。

Abstract: Datacenter GPU power is the binding constraint on LLM serving capacity, and production serving has shifted to prefill/decode (PD) disaggregation. Deploying NVIDIA's Max-Q inference profile on a disaggregated B200 system, we found its realized gain modest (+8.6% tokens/J), model-dependent, and carrying a mean end-to-end latency cost (+5.2%) that throughput-only evaluation does not surface; the profile also applies one setting to prefill and decode GPUs that operate in opposite hardware regimes. We hypothesize that the optimal power setting is a property of the deployed (model, quantization, engine, hardware) combination rather than of the GPU class, that each lane warrants its own profile, and that converting SLO headroom into energy safely requires latency-gated calibration under a runtime SLO guard rather than a fixed recipe. We present a phase-decoupled, model-calibrated controller: the prefill lane runs under an SM-clock window whose floor is a latency guarantee by construction, and the decode lane under a power cap placed by automatic calibration just above a measured throughput/latency cliff. Because a disaggregated decode lane draws flat, memory-bound power, the cap binds continuously, the reactive-overshoot weakness that led POLCA to reject capping is absent, and the GPU's own power manager retains throughput under the cap. On an 8x B200 node serving Qwen3-Coder-480B (FP8) under agentic load, our balanced mode delivers +20.4% tokens/J at +3.5% mean e2e versus +8.6% at +5.2% for Max-Q, a Pareto improvement on both axes. On Qwen3-235B-A22B (NVFP4) every operating mode meets the ITL-p99 SLO in every repetition; both vendor profiles miss it. A decode-actuator A/B shows the calibrated cap beats static clock locks, and a three-day sustained run saves 32.3% of a lane pair's electricity.
