Both models are MoE; a dense model recovers roughly 5x less, so we scope our claims to MoE serving.

</details>


### [247] [Bidirectional Multimodal Fusion of Sky Images and Time-Series for Solar Forecasting with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11135)
*Ken Chen,Maneesha Perera,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Hansani Weeratunge,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SolCloudLLM，一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的多模态光伏功率与全球水平辐照度（GHI）短期预测框架。该方法通过将天空图像块与时间序列块对齐，并采用双向多模态融合生成统一表征，再映射至LLM嵌入空间，有效结合了历史数值数据与地面天空图像的物理信号。在SIRTA和SKIPP'D数据集上的实验表明，SolCloudLLM在所有预测时长下均显著优于现有基线方法，最大相对均方误差降低25.4%；尤其在云层条件下优势明显。此外，在少样本设置下表现最优，而其他深度学习方法性能大幅下降，常被非学习型物理方法超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期光伏功率与全球水平辐照度预测对电网调度与运行至关重要。传统方法依赖历史数值数据，难以预判云层引起的快速变化，而地面天空图像可提供关键的物理补充信号。然而，局部观测数据稀缺且模型对位置敏感，亟需高效、鲁棒且具备强泛化能力的预测方法。尽管大语言模型在时间序列预测中展现出高数据效率与竞争力，但现有方法多为单模态，未充分整合天空图像信息，因此如何构建一个高效融合图像与时间序列的多模态框架成为开放挑战。

Method: 提出SolCloudLLM框架，核心包括：1）将天空图像分块并编码为图像特征；2）将时间序列数据按时间窗口分块并编码为时序特征；3）通过双向多模态融合模块对齐并融合图像与时间序列特征，生成统一表示；4）将融合后的表示映射到大语言模型的嵌入空间，进行最终预测。该设计实现了视觉与数值信号的协同建模。

Result: 在SIRTA和SKIPP'D数据集上，SolCloudLLM在所有预测时长下均实现最低的均方误差（MSE），最大相对降低达25.4%。分层分析显示，多模态融合的优势主要集中在云层条件下的预测。在少样本场景中，SolCloudLLM表现最佳，而其他深度学习方法性能显著下降，甚至低于非学习型物理方法，显示出更强的泛化能力与数据效率。

Conclusion: SolCloudLLM成功构建了一个基于大语言模型的多模态光伏与辐照度预测框架，有效融合天空图像与历史时间序列数据，在多种场景下均表现出卓越性能，尤其在云层变化剧烈和数据稀缺情况下具有显著优势，为未来智能电网中的高精度短期预测提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Short-term photovoltaic (PV) power and global horizontal irradiance (GHI) forecasts are essential for effective dispatch, reserve scheduling, and grid operations. At these forecasting horizons, errors are predominantly driven by cloud induced ramps: relying solely on historical numerical data may struggle to anticipate an incoming cloud, making ground-based sky images a crucial complementary physical signal. Furthermore, forecast performance is highly sensitive to location and local observing conditions, creating a strong need for site-specific data that are often scarce. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated competitive performance and high data efficiency in time-series forecasting. Despite their success, existing LLM-based forecasting methods remain predominantly unimodal, relying primarily on historical numerical time-series data. Effectively incorporating sky imagery into an LLM-based forecasting framework remains under-explored and an open challenge. In this paper, we propose SolCloudLLM, an LLM-based multimodal forecasting framework. SolCloudLLM aligns sky-image patches with time-series patches and fuses their corresponding representations through bidirectional multimodal fusion, yielding a unified representation that is subsequently mapped into the embedding space of an LLM. Extensive experiments on the SIRTA and SKIPP'D datasets demonstrate that SolCloudLLM consistently outperforms the best baseline methods in MSE across all forecasting horizons, achieving a maximum relative MSE reduction of 25.4%. Stratified analysis further indicates that the benefits of multimodal fusion are concentrated primarily under cloudy conditions. Notably, SolCloudLLM achieves the best performance in nearly all few-shot settings, whereas other deep learning baselines experience substantial performance degradation and are frequently outperformed by the non-learning physical method.

</details>


### [248] [LILA: Calibration-Free Structured Pruning of Large Language Models via Latent Spectral Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11163)
*Sankar Behera,Dhruv Singh,Anshika Agnihotri,Raj Kumar Choudhary,Satyadev Ahlawat,Yamuna Prasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LILA提出一种无需训练、校准数据或辅助网络的结构化剪枝方法，通过计算全模型与神经元移除后前馈网络权重矩阵的奇异值分布之间的Kolmogorov-Smirnov距离来评估神经元重要性，实现高效的硬件压缩。在零样本精度上超越PruneNet和SliceGPT，经少量微调后性能接近甚至媲美高度校准的基线方法。理论分析表明其功能失真显著低于随机剪枝，且可动态分配稀疏度预算，在中等压缩下保持生成质量，并揭示单层架构瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法依赖校准数据、梯度计算或大型辅助策略网络，限制了实用性；亟需一种无需额外训练或数据的高效、可扩展的剪枝机制。

Method: 基于奇异值分布的KS距离计算，衡量神经元移除对前馈网络权重矩阵谱特性的影响，构建闭式谱重要性评分规则。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B和Phi-2上，零样本精度优于PruneNet和SliceGPT；经一周期LoRA微调后性能接近高度校准基线（差距<0.48pp）；理论分析显示功能失真减少22倍；动态稀疏分配实现生成能力最优保持并暴露架构瓶颈。

Conclusion: LILA提供了一种高效、无需校准的结构化剪枝范式，兼具卓越性能与理论保障，为大模型压缩提供了新思路。

Abstract: Structured pruning of large language models (LLMs) offers hardware-efficient compression, yet existing methods require calibration data, gradient computation, or large auxiliary policy networks at pruning time. LILA (\emph{Latent-Informed Layer Analysis}) scores neuron importance via the Kolmogorov--Smirnov (KS) distance between empirical singular value distributions of the full and neuron-ablated feed-forward network (FFN) weight matrix, providing a closed-form spectral rule requiring no training, calibration data, or auxiliary network. Without any fine-tuning, LILA surpasses PruneNet (45M-parameter RL policy) by 1.57~pp in zero-shot accuracy on LLaMA-2-7B at 25\% sparsity, and outperforms WikiText-2-calibrated SliceGPT by up to 6.0~pp across all sparsity levels, while preserving the original architecture. After one epoch of LoRA recovery fine-tuning, LILA achieves highly competitive performance, matching the heavily calibrated SliceGPT baseline to within a 0.48~pp margin across LLaMA-2-7B and Phi-2, despite using zero calibration data. A Neural Tangent Kernel analysis confirms a 22$\times$ reduction in functional distortion versus random pruning, providing theoretical grounding for the spectral importance criterion. Finally, extending LILA to dynamically allocate sparsity budgets via KS-scores yields state-of-the-art generative preservation at moderate compression, while uncovering fundamental single-layer architectural bottlenecks at higher compression regimes.

</details>


### [249] [When does a spectral prior help graph learning? Connectivity-loss estimation under road-network disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11166)
*Van-Truong Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出使用图神经网络（GNN）估计多边删除后图的相对代数连通性损失，以在精确谱重新计算与局部近似之间取得实用平衡。比较了GCN、GraphSAGE和边感知MPNN等模型，发现残差GCN在空间失效情况下降低MAE达0.0391，残差GraphSAGE在目标失效下提升0.0257。第二阶微扰改进有限，仅0.0028-0.0053。跨国家迁移结果混合，残差GCN在目标失效中表现良好但空间点估计恶化。模型具备扩展至2万节点的能力，实现一次谱设置与可摊销筛查成本分离。表明谱残差作为结构连通性筛选的归纳偏置虽有效但具领域敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在面对大量同时发生的道路链接中断时，需要一种介于完全精确谱计算与局部近似之间的高效评估方法。传统方法难以兼顾效率与精度，因此亟需一种能够快速估算连通性损失的新机制。

Method: 采用图神经网络（GNN）学习对一阶Fiedler敏感度的有界修正项，用于估计多边删除后的相对代数连通性损失。使用GCN、GraphSAGE和边感知MPNN作为骨干网络，并在合成分裂与真实世界OpenStreetMap（OSM）区域上进行零样本迁移测试。通过对比分析基线方法，评估模型在不同故障模式下的性能。

Result: 残差GCN在空间失效场景下将平均绝对误差（MAE）降低0.0391（95%分层区间：0.0151–0.0662），残差GraphSAGE在目标失效下改善MAE 0.0257（0.0095–0.0446）。第二阶微扰仅带来0.0028–0.0053的改进。跨国家迁移中，残差GCN在目标失效下提升显著（MAE改善0.0622），但在空间点估计上表现下降。模型可扩展至20,000节点规模，实现一次性谱初始化与后续成本摊销。

Conclusion: 谱残差作为一种结构连通性筛查的归纳偏置具有实用价值，但其性能受数据分布影响较大，表现出一定的领域敏感性。该方法在大规模复杂网络中具备良好的可扩展性和应用潜力。

Abstract: Rapid evaluation of many simultaneous road-link disruptions requires a practical compromise between exact spectral recomputation and local approximation. We estimate relative algebraic-connectivity loss after multi-edge deletion using graph neural networks (GNNs) that learn a bounded correction to a first-order Fiedler sensitivity. The study considers independent, spatially clustered, and edge-betweenness-targeted failures, with graph-disjoint synthetic splits and zero-shot transfer to 13 OpenStreetMap (OSM) areas in six countries. GCN, GraphSAGE, and edge-aware MPNN backbones are compared with analytical baselines. In expanded OSM tests, residual GCN improves spatial-failure MAE by 0.0391 (95% hierarchical interval 0.0151-0.0662), while residual GraphSAGE improves targeted-failure MAE by 0.0257 (0.0095-0.0446). Second-order perturbation improves first-order MAE by only 0.0028-0.0053. Correction slopes decrease under targeted transfer, indicating residual shrinkage around systematic prior error. Leave-one-country-out OSM-to-OSM transfer is mixed: residual GCN improves targeted-failure MAE by 0.0622 (0.0169-0.1153) but worsens the spatial point estimate. Sparse scaling extends to 20,000 nodes and separates one-time spectral setup from amortized screening cost. These results characterize the spectral residual as a useful but domain-sensitive inductive bias for structural connectivity screening. Code, cached networks, and reproducibility artifacts are archived at doi:10.5281/zenodo.22307723.

</details>


### [250] [Semi-Tensor Product-Based Multi-Term Randomized T-SVD and Its Visual Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11168)
*Xingchen Xiao,Feng Zhang,Wenjin Qin,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型的三阶张量半张量积（Semi-tensor Product, STP），在t-product框架下通过任意可逆线性变换实现，突破了传统t-product对维度匹配的严格要求，同时保持T-SVD的闭式解特性。基于此，构建多项式半张量积奇异值分解（MSTP-SVD），通过引入多个正交分解项显著提升低秩逼近精度；进一步结合随机投影与幂迭代技术，提出加速的多项目标随机半张量积SVD（MRSTP-SVD）算法，在保证重建精度的同时降低计算开销。实验表明该方法在图像与视频压缩、补全任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有张量奇异值分解（T-SVD）受限于严格的维度兼容性，虽有基于半张量积（STP）的扩展缓解该问题，但其单一项式结构仍导致逼近精度有限，且在处理大规模数据时计算成本过高。因此亟需一种既能灵活适应不同维度、又能高效高精度建模的新方法。

Method: 提出一种基于任意可逆线性变换的新型三阶张量半张量积，构建多项式半张量积奇异值分解（MSTP-SVD），利用多个正交分解项增强逼近能力，并结合随机投影与幂迭代技术设计加速算法（MRSTP-SVD），以平衡精度与效率。

Result: 所提方法在图像与视频压缩、补全任务中均展现出优于现有方法的重构精度和计算效率，尤其在大尺度数据上优势明显。

Conclusion: 本文提出的MRSTP-SVD框架有效解决了传统T-SVD在维度灵活性与计算效率上的瓶颈，为高维视觉数据处理提供了更强大、更高效的工具。

Abstract: Tensor singular value decomposition (T-SVD), which is built upon the tensor-tensor product (t-product), has emerged as a powerful tool for processing high-dimensional visual data such as color images and videos. However, the standard t-product imposes strict dimensional compatibility constraints. Although extensions based on the semi-tensor product (STP) relax this restriction, their single-term formulations still suffer from limited approximation accuracy. Moreover, these deterministic methods incur high computational costs when processing large-scale tensor data. To address these issues, this paper introduces a novel semi-tensor product for third-order tensors under the t-product framework induced by arbitrary invertible linear transforms. The resulting tensor semi-tensor product breaks the rigid dimension matching requirement of the standard t-product, while retaining the closed-form property of T-SVD. Based on this construction, we develop a multi-term semi-tensor product singular value decomposition (MSTP-SVD), which integrates multiple orthogonal decomposition terms to significantly improve low-rank approximation accuracy compared with single-term schemes. To reduce the computational cost of multi-term modeling, we incorporate randomized projection and power iteration techniques into the MSTP-SVD framework, yielding an accelerated multi-term randomized semi-tensor product SVD (MRSTP-SVD) algorithm that achieves a balance between reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency. Experiments on image and video compression and completion tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [251] [Hierarchical Clustering Can Jointly Satisfy Richness, Consistency, and Scale Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11173)
*Daichi Kuroda,Maximilien Dreveton,Matthias Grossglauser,Patrick Thiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究层次聚类是否能同时满足尺度不变性、丰富性和一致性三个自然公理，发现与平面聚类不同，层次聚类的这些公理可同时满足。存在不可数多个满足条件的层次聚类方法（称为可接受方法），并构造了基于分离簇和非二叉单链的方法。这些方法之间存在细化关系，形成偏序集，该集合无最大元且包含不可数多个两两不相容的最大元素，揭示出方法间的多样性；但这种多样性受到约束：所有可接受方法均包含足够分离簇的层次结构，且任意有限个方法共享一个非平凡公共骨架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决聚类缺乏统一定义的问题，特别是探讨在层次聚类中是否能克服Kleinberg不可能定理带来的限制。

Method: 通过形式化层次聚类的尺度不变性、丰富性和一致性公理，证明其联合可满足性；构造具体可接受的层次聚类方法，并分析它们之间的细化关系，建立偏序结构。

Result: 层次聚类的三个公理可同时满足，存在不可数多个可接受方法；这些方法间存在细化关系，形成无最大元且含不可数多个两两不相容最大元素的偏序集；尽管多样性显著，但所有方法共享一个由充分分离簇构成的公共骨架。

Conclusion: 与平面聚类不同，层次聚类能够同时满足尺度不变性、丰富性和一致性公理，展现出丰富的可接受方法体系，其内在结构既体现多样性又受制于共同的骨架约束。

Abstract: Despite its ubiquity, clustering lacks a universally accepted definition of what is a cluster. Kleinberg's Impossibility Theorem formalizes this difficulty by showing that no flat clustering method can simultaneously satisfy three natural axioms: scale invariance, richness, and consistency. In this paper, we ask whether this impossibility persists when the output is a hierarchy rather than a single partition. We show that, in contrast to the flat clustering setting, the hierarchical analog of these axioms are jointly satisfiable. In fact, there exist uncountably many hierarchical clustering methods satisfying these axioms, which we call admissible. We explicitly construct several admissible methods, including methods based on well-separated clusters and a non-binary version of single linkage. For certain pairs of admissible methods, the hierarchy produced by one always refines that produced by the other. This refinement relation defines a partial order on the class of admissible methods. This partially ordered set has no greatest element and contains uncountably many pairwise incompatible maximal elements, revealing substantial diversity among admissible methods. Nevertheless, this diversity is constrained: every admissible method contains a hierarchy of sufficiently well-separated clusters, and every finite collection of admissible methods shares such a nontrivial common backbone.

</details>


### [252] [Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES) under Time-Varying Loss Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11207)
*Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将CONES框架扩展至损失函数随时间变化的场景，研究了在线算法在同时最小化遗憾与移动成本方面的性能。对于凸损失函数，投影近端算法可实现$O(T^{1-β})$的遗憾和$O(T^β)$的移动成本（任意$β\in[0,1)$）；任何弱自适应算法若具有$O(T^β)$遗憾，则其移动成本至少为$Ω(T^{(1-β)/2})$。对于强凸损失函数，投影近端算法可实现常数遗憾和$O(\log T)$移动成本；任何具有亚线性即时遗憾的算法，其移动成本至少为$Ω(\log T)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展原有的CONES模型，使其适用于损失函数随时间动态变化的场景，以更真实地模拟在线优化中的实际问题，并研究在此类动态环境下的算法性能边界。

Method: 采用投影近端算法作为核心方法，结合凸分析与在线学习理论，对不同类型的损失函数（凸与强凸）分别进行性能分析，通过构造下界证明算法的最优性。

Result: 对于凸损失函数，提出算法可实现$O(T^{1-β})$遗憾与$O(T^β)$移动成本，且存在$Ω(T^{(1-β)/2})$的移动成本下界；对于强凸损失函数，算法达到$O(1)$遗憾与$O(\log T)$移动成本，且存在$Ω(\log T)$移动成本下界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了动态损失函数下在线凸优化中遗憾与移动成本之间的权衡关系，证明了所提算法的近似最优性，并揭示了在强凸情形下可实现近乎理想的性能。

Abstract: Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES)} was introduced in \cite{CONESVaze} where the objective function \(f\) remains fixed but the feasible region evolves over time as a nested sequence \(S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T\). The goal of an online algorithm is to simultaneously minimize the regret with respect to hindsight static optimal benchmark and the total movement cost $M_\cA(T)$ while ensuring feasibility at all times. CONES is an optimization-oriented generalization of the well-known \emph{nested convex body chasing} (NCBC). In this paper, we extend CONES to allow for loss functions $f_t'$s to also change over time. When all loss functions are convex, we show that the projected proximal algorithm achieves $O(T^{1-β}), O(T^β)$ simultaneous regret and movement cost, respectively, for any $β\in [0,1)$, over a time horizon of $T$. We also show that any {\it weakly adaptive} online algorithm with $O(T^β)$ regret has a movement cost of $Ω\left(T^{\frac{1-β}{2}}\right)$ for any $β\in [0,1)$. When all loss functions are strongly convex, we show that the projected proximal algorithm simultaneously achieves $O(1)$ regret and a movement cost of $O(\log T)$. To complement this, we show that any online algorithm with sublinear {\it anytime} regret has a movement cost of $Ω\left(\log T\right)$.

</details>


### [253] [REVA: Reusable Evidence View Aggregation for Context-Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11209)
*Tuan Nguyen,Qiran Hu,Banruo Liu,Khoa D. Doan,Kok-Seng Wong,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REVA提出一种从数据挖掘角度出发的RAG压缩框架，通过聚合历史查询-文档-模型交互生成可复用的证据视图，有效降低压缩开销并提升生成质量。相比现有方法，REVA在多个基准上实现1.0–5.8分的性能提升，压缩延迟降低5.3至15.6倍，增加延迟不足40毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG压缩方法存在独立处理每个查询、依赖辅助模型或重写、引入在线开销等问题，导致压缩收益被抵消。需要更高效、低延迟且可复用的压缩机制。

Method: REVA通过挖掘目标生成器的历史注意力轨迹，构建基于文档的、预算无关的评分存储；将词元级注意力映射到可读词单元，跨重复文档访问聚合重要性，生成保持文档顺序与标准RAG接口的预算相关纯文本视图。

Result: 在四个代表性基准和现代LLM上，REVA相比现有先进方法提升生成质量1.0–5.8点，压缩开销降低5.3–15.6倍，推理延迟增加小于40毫秒。

Conclusion: REVA通过历史交互数据的聚合实现了高效、低延迟、可复用的RAG压缩，显著优于现有方法，在性能与效率间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive large language model (LLM) applications by conditioning generation on retrieved documents, but longer contexts increase latency, key-value (KV) cache memory, and token cost. Post-retrieval compression can reduce this cost, yet existing compressors often operate independently for each query, rely on auxiliary models or rewriting, and introduce online overhead that can offset the benefit of shorter prompts. We revisit RAG compression from a data-mining perspective by aggregating historical query--document--model interactions into reusable evidence views. We first show that modern compressors have unstable gains over simple truncation and can add substantial inference-time latency. We then propose Reusable Evidence View Aggregation (REVA), a framework that mines the target generator's historical attention traces into a document-keyed, budget-agnostic score store. REVA maps token-level attention to readable word units, aggregates importance across repeated document accesses, and renders budget-specific plain-text views that preserve document order and the standard RAG interface. Across four representative benchmarks and modern LLMs, REVA improves generation quality by 1.0--5.8 points over existing advances, while reducing compression overhead by a factor of 5.3 to 15.6, adding less than 40 ms of latency.

</details>


### [254] [Legible Failures: Detecting and Repairing In-Context Binding Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11216)
*Manas Venkata Sai Ravulapalli,Samrath Singh Chadha,Abhinav M. Hari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了语言模型在实体-义务绑定任务中出错的原因，发现错误可能源于未能使用已有信息而非缺乏信息。通过线性探针从冻结的隐藏状态中恢复正确绑定，结果显示探针在模型出错时仍能显著提升准确率。进一步分析表明，基于探针输出不一致性的评分可有效提升错误检测能力，且无需真实标签即可通过引导残差流改进模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在实体-义务绑定任务中出错的根本原因，区分是信息缺失还是信息未被有效利用，并探索如何通过探针技术检测和纠正模型错误。

Method: 在16个公开检查点上进行三重种子评估；训练集上拟合探针，验证集上选择最佳层，测试集上报告结果；使用反事实分析排除令牌存在性和时效性的影响；构建基于探针输出差异的评分以改进错误检测；通过无标签引导残差流实现模型性能提升。

Result: 在模型出错的样本中，探针准确率显著高于基线（+0.196），探针一致性评分使错误检测的AUROC提升+0.079；原始探针置信度无明显改善；残差流引导使8个模型平均准确率提升+0.168。

Conclusion: 探针不仅能有效检测语言模型的错误，还能在无真实标签的情况下通过干预残差流实现性能提升，表明探针在上下文绑定任务中具有可操作性，为模型纠错提供了新路径。

Abstract: A wrong answer does not show whether the model lacked the needed information or held it and failed to use it. On an entity-obligation binding task, a language model can emit an incorrect prompt-supplied binding while a linear probe can recover the correct one from its frozen hidden state. We measure how often this occurs across 16 public checkpoints, each evaluated with three seeds. We fit a probe on a training fold, select its layer on a validation fold, and report results on a disjoint test fold. On the trials each model gets wrong, probe accuracy exceeds the strict present-obligation baseline, 1/K = 0.125, by +0.196 (95% CI [+0.101, +0.296], bootstrapped over models). A query-entity counterfactual rules out token presence and recency. A score built from the sign of probe-output disagreement improves failure detection over the model's own confidence by +0.079 AUROC (95% CI [+0.036, +0.126]). Raw probe confidence gives no measurable improvement over model confidence. Steering the residual stream toward the probe-decoded binding, with no gold label, raises accuracy on all eight models tested by a mean of +0.168 (95% CI [+0.066, +0.280]). Where recent studies report that probe-detected errors are resistant to interventions, we find that in-context binding is a setting in which probes are actionable.

</details>


### [255] [Polyhedral Geometry of Time-to-First-Spike Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11227)
*Manjot Singh,Guido Montúfar,Gitta Kutyniok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究脉冲神经网络（SNN）的表达能力，聚焦于基于首次脉冲时间的模型。在输入-输出映射为连续分段线性的情况下，每个神经元的放电时间可表示为具有大量受限仿射片段的maxout形式。通过定义因果区域为具有固定因果集合的多面体区域，推导出浅层和多层前馈脉冲网络中最大因果区域数的上下界。理论与实验结果表明，脉冲网络能对输入空间生成比传统ReLU网络更丰富的划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索脉冲神经网络在异步、事件驱动计算中的表达能力，揭示其相对于传统前馈神经网络的优势。

Method: 分析时间到首次脉冲模型，利用因果可行性约束建模神经元放电行为，通过多面体几何方法推导因果区域的数量边界，并结合理论与实验验证表达能力。

Result: 脉冲神经网络能够产生比传统ReLU网络更复杂的输入空间划分，具有更高的表达能力；其放电时间具有maxout-like结构，包含指数级数量的受限仿射片段。

Conclusion: 脉冲神经网络在表达能力上优于传统前馈网络，尤其在处理时序和事件驱动任务方面具有显著潜力。

Abstract: We study the expressivity of spiking neural networks, which provide a natural framework for asynchronous, event-driven computation complementary to conventional feedforward neural networks. We consider the time-to-first-spike model in a setting for which the input-output map is continuous and piecewise linear, with affine pieces governed by causal feasibility constraints that determine which presynaptic spikes occur before a neuron fires. We first show that each neuron's firing time admits a maxout-like representation with exponentially many, highly constrained affine pieces. We then formalize causal regions as polyhedral regions with fixed causal sets and derive upper and lower bounds on the maximal number of causal regions in both shallow and multilayer feedforward spiking networks. Our theoretical and experimental results show that spiking networks can generate richer partitions of the input space than conventional feedforward ReLU networks.

</details>


### [256] [Solving Few-Shot Multiobjective Multitask Optimization via Iterative Sequential Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11228)
*Tingyang Wei,Haofeng Wu,Ananda Phan Iman,Zhao Wei,Jiao Liu,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代顺序迁移（IST）方法，以解决多任务优化（MTO）中因评估预算有限导致精英解分布难以识别的瓶颈问题。通过将MTO建模为一系列顺序迁移优化问题，每次集中资源优化一个目标，并引入基于似然的任务优先级机制，选择最适宜进行知识融合的任务，从而提升迁移效率。实验结果在基准和真实世界问题上验证了该方法在严苛预算下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在少样本优化场景下，由于评估预算受限，难以准确识别各任务间的精英解分布，阻碍了有效的知识迁移；尤其在多目标多任务优化中，需逼近连续的帕累托流形，进一步加剧了挑战。

Method: 将多任务优化建模为一系列顺序迁移优化问题，每轮聚焦单一目标；设计基于似然的任务优先级机制，动态选择最适合作知识迁移的任务，实现高效评估资源分配。

Result: 在多个基准测试和真实世界问题上，所提方法在严格评估预算下显著提升了优化性能，证明了其在少样本条件下的有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: Iterative Sequential Transfer（IST）有效缓解了多任务优化中因评估资源有限导致的知识迁移瓶颈，尤其适用于多目标、少样本场景，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Applying knowledge transfer across multiple optimization tasks, multitask optimization (MTO) emerges as a promising approach to solving synergistic optimization tasks simultaneously. However, the development of effective knowledge transfer mechanisms in MTO fundamentally relies on aligning elite solution distributions across tasks. This dependency creates a critical bottleneck in few-shot optimization regimes, as restricted evaluation budgets impede the identification of elite solution distributions required for beneficial transfer. This challenge is exacerbated in multiobjective multitask problems, where each optimizer must approximate a continuous Pareto manifold rather than a single optimal point. This paper introduces Iterative Sequential Transfer (IST) to circumvent this bottleneck. We model MTO as a sequence of sequential transfer optimization problems, concentrating evaluations on a single target per iteration. We propose a likelihood-informed task prioritization mechanism to maximize transfer utility by identifying the task most likely ready for knowledge integration. Empirical results on benchmark and real-world problems verify the effectiveness of the proposed method under tight budgets.

</details>


### [257] [MUtE: A Dual Framework for Concept Erasure and Counterfactual Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11253)
*Antoine Saillenfest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的概念擦除方法，通过理论最优边界推导出具有确定性双反事实映射的擦除函数，结合几何上符合现代语言模型中概念表现的平移偏差约束，实现了概念擦除与反事实生成的无缝切换，并在下游算法公平性和反事实文本生成任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在概念擦除中难以平衡去偏效果与信息保留，且缺乏理论指导与实际应用之间的桥梁。本文旨在解决这一问题，提升模型在公平性和可解释性方面的表现。

Method: 基于概念擦除的最优边界推导新擦除函数，引入平移偏差约束以匹配语言模型中概念的几何特性，设计可实现的概念擦除与反事实生成框架。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在提升算法公平性及生成高质量反事实文本方面表现优异，有效实现了概念信息的可控擦除与反事实推理。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架成功连接了理论最优性与实际表示学习，为概念擦除和反事实生成提供了高效、可解释的新范式。

Abstract: Erasing concept-specific information from representations has been proven useful for mitigating bias or interpreting model decisions. The joint objective is to transform the original representations such that the target concept becomes unpredictable, while maximally preserving concept-unrelated information. In this work, we revisit the optimal bounds of concept erasure to derive a novel class of erasure functions that naturally induce a deterministic, dual counterfactual mapping. Bridging the gap between theoretical optimality and practical representation learning, we design an implementation that imposes a translational bias on counterfactual trajectories - a constraint that aligns with how many concepts geometrically manifest in modern language models. Our framework enables seamless navigation between concept erasure and counterfactual generation. We empirically demonstrate its efficacy in improving downstream algorithmic fairness and generating counterfactual texts.

</details>


### [258] [A Dynamic Fusion Large Language Model for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11314)
*Xue Qiu,Jianli Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于交通流量预测的动态融合大语言模型（DF-LLM），通过结合时空嵌入、时空融合模块和大语言模型主干网络，有效捕捉多尺度时空特征与空间拓扑关系，提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络受限于单一特征建模，而大语言模型在空间拓扑信息捕捉和时空相关性挖掘方面表现不足，亟需一种能够兼顾高效建模与全局依赖性的新方法。

Method: 设计了包含时空嵌入模块、时空融合模块和大语言模型主干的DF-LLM架构；引入图卷积融合空间拓扑与动态依赖，采用差异化参数适配策略提升训练效率与数据适应性，并通过上下文聚合注意力机制增强全局依赖，利用残差连接缓解深层网络梯度消失问题。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明，DF-LLM在各项指标上均优于现有方法，显著提升了交通流量预测的准确性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DF-LLM通过深度融合时空特征与大语言模型能力，为智能交通系统中的交通流预测提供了高效且精准的新解决方案。

Abstract: Traffic flow prediction is a core supporting technology for intelligent transportation systems. It uses historical data to infer future traffic dynamics in specific areas, thereby helping to alleviate congestion and improve resource allocation efficiency. Traditional neural networks struggle to break through accuracy limits due to their reliance on singular feature modeling, while large language models (LLMs) suffer from insufficient capture of spatial topological information and mining spatiotemporal correlation. This study proposes a Dynamic Fusion Large Language Model (DF-LLM) for traffic flow prediction. The model incorporates three core components: spatiotemporal embedding module, spatiotemporal fusion module, and LLM backbone. The spatiotemporal embedding module enables synergistic representation of multi-scale spatiotemporal features. The spatiotemporal fusion module integrates spatial topology and dynamic dependencies via graph convolution. The LLM backbone adopts a differentiated parameter adaptation strategy to balance training efficiency and traffic data adaptability. Additionally, it introduces a context aggregation attention module to strengthens global dependencies. More importantly, the LLM backbone takes the residual connections to mitigate the gradient vanishing in deep networks. Experiments show that DF-LLM has achieved better performance by comparing the metrics on all the four datasets.

</details>


### [259] [Estimating Inconsistency Response Surfaces under Uncertainty in Cyber-Physical System Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11331)
*Johannes Mäkelburg,Tim Schwabe,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于干预响应建模的方法，用于系统性地探索、分析和解释在大规模不确定性空间中的网络物理系统（CPS）不一致性问题。通过盐德尔采样与多保真度蒙特卡洛估计生成数据，并训练代理模型以快速预测不一致性，显著缩短评估时间（从毫秒降至微秒），支持在有限计算预算下进行大量响应面评估。在此基础上，开展敏感性分析以识别主要不确定性来源，并提出基于梯度的一致性修复方法，实现最小干预恢复一致性。实验验证了该方法在48个场景和10个CPS领域中的有效性，为不确定性感知的一致性管理提供了可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽能判断特定不确定性配置下系统的兼容性，但难以系统化地探索、分析和解释大规模不确定性空间中的不一致性问题。随着传感器噪声、模型抽象等不确定性因素的引入，系统各模型间的一致性容易被破坏，亟需一种高效、可扩展的分析与修复机制。

Method: 将不一致性建模为干预响应问题；采用盐德尔采样生成输入样本，结合多保真度蒙特卡洛估计构建干预-响应数据集；训练代理模型直接预测不一致性；基于学习到的响应面进行敏感性分析和梯度驱动的一致性修复。

Result: 代理模型精度接近传统蒙特卡洛方法，但评估速度提升数个数量级（毫秒→微秒），支持海量响应面评估；敏感性分析成功识别关键不确定性因素；梯度一致性修复方法能有效找到最小干预策略以恢复系统一致性。

Conclusion: 不一致性在不确定性下的学习、分析与修复可通过响应面建模实现，该方法为网络物理系统开发中不确定性感知的一致性管理提供了高效且可扩展的技术基础。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) are commonly represented through multiple interconnected models. During development, CPS consistency requires that shared model elements remain compatible across these models. Uncertainty, for example, due to sensor noise or model abstraction, changes the admissible values of model elements and can introduce inconsistencies, i.e., situations in which models can no longer be jointly satisfied. While existing approaches can determine consistency for a given uncertainty configuration, they provide limited support for systematically exploring, analyzing, and explaining inconsistency across large uncertainty spaces. We address this challenge by reformulating inconsistency as an intervention response modeling problem. Using Saltelli sampling and multi-fidelity Monte Carlo estimation, we generate intervention-response datasets and train a surrogate model that directly predicts inconsistency from the propagated uncertainty geometry. Experiments on 48 scenarios and 10 CPS domains show that the surrogate matches Monte Carlo estimates while reducing evaluation time from milliseconds to microseconds, enabling orders-of-magnitude more response-surface evaluations within fixed computational budgets. Building on the learned response surfaces, we perform sensitivity analysis to identify dominant uncertainty drivers and introduce a gradient-based consistency recourse method to determine minimal uncertainty interventions that restore consistency. The results show that inconsistency under uncertainty can be effectively learned, analyzed, and repaired through response-surface modeling, providing a scalable foundation for uncertainty-aware consistency management in CPS development.

</details>


### [260] [Reification as a Transferable Vocabulary: Zero-Shot Link Prediction with Vanilla GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11347)
*Camille Pradel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将知识图谱输入显式表示为节点的新型方法，通过六种固定元关系连接事实与主体、客体及关系类型，使关系类型作为共享匿名节点而非模型参数。在此表示下，五个标准GNN（如GAT）在单个知识图谱上训练30分钟，即可零样本迁移至40个归纳链接预测基准，表现媲美专门设计的ULTRA基础模型。该方法还可扩展至关系数据库，初步实验显示其在无值、无文本、无上下文标签情况下仍能有效识别外键目标。代码、模型检查点与评估流程均已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱基础模型（如ULTRA）依赖专用架构硬编码转移机制，限制了通用性与可解释性。本文旨在将转移机制从架构中移出，转而通过重新表示输入图来实现更灵活、可泛化的零样本迁移能力。

Method: 将每个事实视为一个节点，并通过六种固定元关系连接至其主体、客体和关系类型；关系类型作为匿名共享节点处理，不作为模型参数；使用标准GNN（如GAT、GraphSAGE等）在单一知识图谱上训练，进行零样本跨图迁移。

Result: 所提方法在40个归纳链接预测基准上实现了与专用基础模型ULTRA相当的性能，且仅需单图训练30分钟；在关系数据库上的初步实验也显示出显著优于随机初始化和度控制的表现。

Conclusion: 通过重新表示知识图谱，将转移机制从架构转移到表示层，可实现高效、通用的零样本迁移学习，且适用于多种数据形式（如关系数据库），具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Knowledge graph foundation models such as ULTRA achieve zero-shot link prediction on unseen graphs through dedicated architectures that hard-code a transfer mechanism. In this work we move that mechanism out of the architecture and into the representation, by \emph{reifying} the input graph: every fact becomes a node, connected to its subject, object, and relation type through a fixed vocabulary of six meta-relations, with relation types as anonymous shared nodes rather than model parameters. On this representation, five textbook GNNs (GAT, GINE with sum and with mean+max aggregation, GraphSAGE, R-GCN), each trained on a single knowledge graph of 4,245 triples for 30 minutes on one NVIDIA A100, transfer zero-shot to 40 inductive link-prediction benchmarks. The best of them, an off-the-shelf GAT, matches ULTRA, a dedicated foundation model pretrained on three graphs, across ULTRA's own evaluation suite. The same fixed vocabulary extends to relational databases, a row becoming an entity and a foreign-key column a relation type; a preliminary probe on two unseen databases, with no cell values, schema text or in-context labels, shows a model of this family pretrained on three knowledge graphs ranking foreign-key targets far above random-initialization and degree controls. We release the code, the checkpoints, and the evaluation pipeline for all 40 benchmarks.

</details>


### [261] [Local Robustness Quantification for Naive Bayes Classifiers and Generative Forests: a General Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11366)
*Adrián Detavernier,Jasper De Bock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算两类生成式分类器（朴素贝叶斯和生成森林）预测鲁棒性的方法，这些分类器基于概率图模型（PGM）。通过扰动PGM的局部模型，定义了预测鲁棒性为在不改变预测结果的前提下允许的最大分布扰动。研究考虑了epsilon-污染、总变差距离和卡方散度球等扰动形式，并在基准数据集上验证了方法的有效性。结果表明，预测的鲁棒性值可作为其可信度的良好指标，并与其他可信度指标进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高生成式分类器预测的可信度评估能力，通过量化预测对输入扰动的敏感性来判断其可靠性。

Method: 基于概率图模型（PGM）的生成式分类器，通过在局部模型上施加不同类型的扰动（如epsilon-污染、总变差距离、卡方散度），计算预测在扰动下保持不变的最大范围，从而定义并计算鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上验证了方法的有效性，发现预测的鲁棒性值与预测的可信度高度相关，且优于或可媲美其他现有可信度指标。

Conclusion: 提出的鲁棒性量化方法能够有效评估生成式分类器预测的可信度，为模型决策提供了可解释的依据。

Abstract: We provide methods for calculating the robustness of the predictions of two types of generative classifiers whose underlying distribution is a Probabilistic Graphical Model (PGM): naive Bayes classifiers and generative forests (a probabilistic extension of random forests). Following the paradigm of robustness quantification, we define the robustness of a prediction as the extent to which the distribution of the classifier can be perturbed without changing this prediction. We consider perturbations obtained by varying the local models of the PGMs within general neighborhoods and focus in particular on epsilon-contamination, total variation distance and chi-squared divergence balls. We test our methods on benchmark datasets, demonstrate that the robustness value of a prediction serves as an indicator for its trustworthiness and compare our approach with other such indicators.

</details>


### [262] [Prevalence Determines Precision:Silent Contamination in Detector-Defined Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11449)
*Jia Huang,Yankai Wan,Yangjun Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了机器学习数据集构建中因贝叶斯效应导致的精度偏差问题，指出检测器质量并非唯一决定因素，真实事件在候选池中的先验比例起关键作用。通过对比独立标注数据与检测器生成的数据，发现高幻影率池的精度转移会导致严重误差（+422%），而贝叶斯模型可精确预测所有情况。检测信号是真实事件与幻影成分的精确凸组合，幻影数量远超真实事件，且污染具有检测器继承的形状特征，非随机噪声。不同估计器对污染响应不同：某些统计量被稀释，另一些被放大，常见归一化方法可能导致期望不存在，产生不可靠结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习数据集常依赖检测器筛选候选样本，但其标签精度受候选池中真实事件先验比例影响显著，传统方法忽略这一贝叶斯效应，导致评估偏差和错误推断。本研究旨在揭示并量化这种由先验分布引起的系统性误差及其对模型评估的影响。

Method: 使用同一检测器在三个不同候选池上运行，获取检测事件集；同时利用独立官方索引对每个检测项进行真实/幻影标注。通过比较检测结果与独立标注，分析幻影率差异，并验证贝叶斯模型预测能力。进一步分析检测响应曲线的构成，识别真实与幻影成分的线性组合关系，评估不同估计器在污染下的表现。

Result: 三个池的幻影率分别为81.7%、9.0%和0.0%，精度转移预测误差高达+422%，而贝叶斯模型预测误差小于3.3%。检测响应为真实与幻影成分的精确凸组合（残差1.1e-16），幻影数（473）远超真实事件（308）。不同估计器对污染响应相反：一种被稀释，另一种被放大；常见归一化方法在相同335事件下给出0.40，而稳健估计器仅得0.10，表明前者期望可能不存在。

Conclusion: 数据集精度不仅取决于检测器性能，更受候选池中真实事件先验比例的深刻影响。忽视贝叶斯效应将导致严重评估偏差。幻影污染是一种具有检测器特性的结构性信号，而非随机噪声。应避免使用期望不存在的归一化估计器，推荐基于贝叶斯建模与稳健统计的评估方法。

Abstract: Many ML datasets are constructed by running a detector, heuristic, or model over candidate pools; accepted items become labels. Dataset precision is then governed by true-positive prevalence in each pool via Bayes, not solely by detector quality. Using one instrument and period, we hold a detector-defined event dataset plus an independent official index labeling every detected item as real or phantom. One detector, three pools yield phantom rates 81.7%, 9.0%, and 0.0%. Transferring precision from the two high-rate pools to the low-rate pool predicts 0.955 versus measured 0.183, a +422% error; the Bayes expression predicts all three within 3.3%. The detected response curve is an exact convex combination of a true-event and a phantom component (residual 1.1e-16), with phantoms outnumbering true events 473 to 308, so contamination is a second signal with detector-inherited shape, not additive noise. Contamination direction depends on the estimator: on identical windows one statistic is diluted and another inflated because its denominator is also contaminated. A common normalization turns the estimator into a mean of ratios whose expectation need not exist; on the same 335 events it returns 0.40 where the well-defined estimator returns 0.10.

</details>


### [263] [Combining Synthetic and Real Data for Low-Resource Historical OCR: A Manchu Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11495)
*Yan Hon Michael Chung,Hanlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在低资源历史文本光学字符识别（OCR）中，如何结合合成数据与真实历史数据。基于6万张合成和20,306张真实清代满文词图像，评估了三种预训练视觉-语言模型（VLMs）和一个紧凑的卷积循环神经网络（CRNN），在四种训练策略下的表现：仅合成、仅真实、联合训练及顺序合成到真实训练。引入真实数据后，最佳配置的词准确率提升至95.09%至96.28%，而纯合成模型最高仅达87.92%。合成数据显著提升三类VLM性能，对CRNN则取决于训练目标。联合与顺序训练在实际流程下表现相近。即使模型规模较小，一旦使用真实数据，CRNN也能达到领先水平。此外，通过投票机制结合多个强模型，准确率可达98.27%，并利用18世纪满文词典作为判别标准解决分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 满语是清代（1636–1912）的主要语言之一，如今濒临灭绝，其大量档案资料虽已数字化，但难以大规模搜索与分析。现有研究表明，仅用合成满文词图像训练的视觉-语言模型在真实文献上可达到87.4%的词准确率，仍存在显著的合成到真实差距。因此亟需探索如何有效融合合成与真实历史数据以提升低资源场景下的OCR性能。

Method: 采用60,000张合成与20,306张真实历史满文词图像，评估三种预训练视觉-语言模型（VLMs）和一个紧凑的卷积循环神经网络（CRNN）。对比四种训练策略：合成仅用、真实仅用、合成-真实联合训练、以及顺序合成到真实训练。所有实验遵循统一的检查点选择与档案评估协议，以确保可比性。进一步通过模型投票与词典规则进行错误修正，提升最终识别精度。

Result: 引入真实训练图像后，最优配置的词准确率提升至95.09%–96.28%，远高于纯合成模型的87.92%上限。合成数据对三类VLM均有显著增益，而对CRNN的影响则依赖于训练目标。联合与顺序训练在实际应用中表现相当。紧凑的CRNN在接入真实数据后亦能达到领先性能，表明模型规模并非决定因素。通过多模型投票，准确率进一步提升至98.27%，并借助18世纪满文词典实现分歧的有效仲裁。

Conclusion: 在低资源历史文本OCR中，将合成数据与真实历史数据结合可显著提升识别性能。真实数据的引入是关键突破，能大幅缩小合成与真实之间的差距。模型架构虽有差异，但通过合理训练策略与集成方法，即使是轻量级模型也可达到顶尖水平。最终通过模型投票与词典辅助，实现了接近人工标注精度的高可靠性识别。

Abstract: Manchu, now critically endangered, was one of the principal languages of the Qing empire (1636-1912), and its extensive archival record is increasingly digitized but remains difficult to search and analyze at scale. Previous work showed that vision-language models (VLMs) trained only on synthetic Manchu word images can reach 87.4% word accuracy on real Qing manuscripts and prints, leaving a substantial synthetic-to-real gap. This study examines how synthetic and real historical training data should be combined for low-resource OCR. Using 60,000 synthetic and 20,306 real historical word images, we evaluate three pretrained VLMs and a compact convolutional recurrent neural network (CRNN) under four regimes: synthetic-only, real-only, joint synthetic-real, and sequential synthetic-to-real training, following a common checkpoint-selection and archival evaluation protocol. Introducing real training images raises the leading configurations to between 95.09% and 96.28% word accuracy, while no synthetic-only configuration exceeds 87.92%. Synthetic supplementation substantially improves all three VLMs, whereas its marginal effect for the CRNN is sensitive to the training objective. Joint and sequential training yield broadly similar archival accuracy under the tested practical pipelines. A compact CRNN also reaches the leading performance range once real images are available, showing that model scale alone does not determine recognition accuracy. Finally, complementary errors among strong recognizers allow voting to raise accuracy to 98.27% without additional training, while an eighteenth-century Manchu dictionary provides a principled rule for adjudicating disagreements.

</details>


### [264] [DeFiFlowBench: Benchmarking and Improving Safe Executability in Natural-Language DeFi Workflow Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11504)
*Abhinav Rajeev Kumar,Harshit Arora,Varun Singh,Manikandan Nanjappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeFiFlowBench 是一个包含 207 个团队撰写的自然语言 DeFi 工作流提示的基准测试，用于评估图覆盖率、配置完整性和声明的安全性谓词，并在本地 EVM 上测试支持的交易配置。直接、受限和少样本提示在固定 5% 价格冲击上限下产生每配置 14-19 次不安全执行。基于报价推导的滑点限制无法防止订单本身的价差影响。提出 Koan-Safe，结合仅提示意图解析器、可替换生成器和带有默认安全参数的结构修复。在 75 个保留的工作流提示上，其混合变体在静态安全代理上得分 0.67，优于最佳基线的 0.33。Koan-Safe 在保存的基准输出中未记录任何不安全执行。匹配候选消融实验显示，当强制机制关闭时产生 14-17 次不安全执行。额外测试揭示默认注入的局限性：宽松的现有阈值仍可能授权不安全交易。单独评估的策略上限解决了 36 个诊断网格中的这一问题。结果支持显式交易保护和基于执行的评估，同时区分声明安全与普遍保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 DeFi 工作流在结构上有效，但仍可能授权高成本交易。当前方法在自然语言到 DeFi 工作流的合成中缺乏对安全性的真实保障，尤其在价格冲击和滑点控制方面存在漏洞。需要更可靠的评估基准和更有效的安全防护机制。

Method: 引入 DeFiFlowBench 基准，评估多种提示策略下的工作流合成质量；设计 Koan-Safe 系统，融合提示解析、可替换生成器与结构修复，结合默认安全参数实现动态校验；通过消融实验和诊断网格验证安全机制的有效性。

Result: Koan-Safe 在静态安全代理上得分 0.67（优于基线 0.33），且在所有保留测试中无一次不安全执行；消融实验表明安全机制至关重要；策略上限能有效解决默认注入的局限性。

Conclusion: 显式交易保护和基于执行的评估是必要的，仅依赖声明安全不足以确保实际安全；需结合结构修复与动态验证机制以实现可靠的安全保障。

Abstract: A structurally valid DeFi workflow can still authorize a costly trade. We introduce DeFiFlowBench, a benchmark of 207 team-authored prompts for natural-language DeFi workflow synthesis. It measures graph coverage, configuration completeness, and declared safety predicates, then tests supported trade configurations on a local EVM. Direct, constrained, and few-shot prompting produce 14-19 unsafe held-out executions per configuration under a fixed 5% price-impact cap. A slippage bound derived from a quote does not prevent the price impact of the order itself. We propose Koan-Safe, which combines a prompt-only intent parser, a replaceable generator, and structural repair with default safety parameters. On 75 held-out workflow prompts, its hybrid variant scores 0.67 on the static safety proxy, compared with 0.33 for the best baseline. Koan-Safe records no unsafe executions on the saved benchmark outputs. A matched-candidate ablation produces 14-17 unsafe executions when enforcement is disabled. Additional tests expose the limits of default injection: permissive existing thresholds can still authorize unsafe trades. A separately evaluated policy cap addresses this failure on a 36-case diagnostic grid. These results support explicit trade protections and execution-based evaluation, while distinguishing declared safety from a general guarantee.

</details>


### [265] [Generalized Score Matching for Parameter Estimation on Convex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11521)
*Nishanth Shetty,Saisuchith Mahajan,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从最小概率流（MPF）学习出发，构造性地推导了在R^d的凸子集上的广义得分匹配目标函数，展示了经典得分匹配及针对非负数据的域适应变体如何自然地融入该框架。所提出的客观指标是二阶的恰当局部评分规则，理论上保证了在最小化时可恢复真实密度。对于指数族模型，建立了目标函数的凸性以及有限样本估计量的一致性。实验验证了在分区函数解析不可解的约束域上基于广义得分匹配的估计器性能，并展示了其在指数族模型参数估计和生成建模中的广泛应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最大似然估计在未归一化模型中需计算并微分分区函数，这在许多情况下不可行；得分匹配提供了一种避免依赖归一化常数的替代方法，但现有方法在某些问题设置下存在局限性。本文旨在通过从最小概率流出发构建更通用、理论严谨且适用于多种场景的广义得分匹配框架。

Method: 从最小概率流（MPF）学习出发，通过构造性推导得到广义得分匹配的目标函数。利用数学分析证明该目标函数为二阶恰当局部评分规则，进一步在指数族模型假设下证明其凸性与估计一致性。通过实验对比不同约束域下的估计性能，并应用于指数族模型参数估计和生成建模任务。

Result: 提出的方法不仅统一了经典得分匹配与域适应变体，还提供了严格的理论保障：目标函数最小化能恢复真实密度，指数族模型下目标函数凸且估计一致。实验表明该方法在分区函数不可解析的约束域上表现良好，适用于参数估计与生成建模等广泛任务。

Conclusion: 广义得分匹配框架具有更强的理论基础与应用灵活性，能够有效处理未归一化模型的参数估计问题，在多种实际场景中展现出优越性能，为复杂概率模型的学习提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Maximum likelihood (ML) estimation is a principled and statistically efficient approach for learning probabilistic models. However, for unnormalized models, ML estimation requires evaluating the partition function and differentiating through it, which may not always be tractable. Score matching provides a practically viable alternative that circumvents this obstacle by fitting the score in a way that eliminates dependence on the normalizing constant. We derive the generalized score matching objective on a convex subset of $\mathbb{R}^{d}$ constructively starting from Minimum Probability Flow (MPF) learning, and show how classical score matching as well as domain-adapted variants for non-negative data arise naturally within the proposed framework. We show that the resulting objective is a {\it proper local scoring rule} of second-order, which provides the theoretical guarantee that the true density is recovered when the objective is minimized. Furthermore, for a model belonging to the exponential family, we establish convexity of the objective together with consistency of the finite-sample estimator under standard regularity conditions. Our derivation sheds new light on the scope and applicability of generalized score matching in various problem settings. We compare generalized score matching-based estimators on constrained domains, where the partition function is analytically intractable. We provide experimental results on parameter estimation for model densities belonging to the exponential family defined over convex subsets of $\mathbb{R}^{d}$, and a generative modeling use-case to demonstrate broader applicability of the proposed generalized score matching framework.

</details>


### [266] [Particle GFlowNets: Rethinking Generative Marginalization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11538)
*Tiago da Silva,Diego Mesquita,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Generative Marginalization Models (MaMs) are shown to be equivalent to Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets), and extended to non-autoregressive generation via a rejuvenation criterion based on the Gelman-Rubin statistic, leading to faster training convergence in large combinatorial spaces.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To unify MaMs and GFlowNets under a common framework and improve sampling efficiency by enabling non-autoregressive generation with faster convergence.

Method: Demonstrate equivalence between MaMs and GFlowNets, introduce an automatic full-state rejuvenation strategy using the Gelman-Rubin statistic, and apply it to non-autoregressive generative processes.

Result: Particle GFlowNets achieve significantly accelerated training in large combinatorial spaces due to improved sampling dynamics and faster convergence.

Conclusion: MaMs and GFlowNets are fundamentally equivalent, and extending MaMs with rejuvenation-based non-autoregressive sampling leads to more efficient training in complex discrete distributions.

Abstract: Generative Marginalization Models (MaMs) have been recently introduced as efficient neural sampling models for any-order autoregressive modelling of discrete distributions. By learning both the marginal and conditional probabilities of a persistent-block Gibbs sampler, MaMs enable fast posterior evaluation with a single neural network forward pass. While prior work has considered MaMs to be distinct from Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets), a well-established paradigm for inference in discrete stochastic models, we show that they are equivalent. Then, we also extend MaMs' sampling strategy to non-autoregressive generative processes. In particular, we describe an automatic criterion for full-state rejuvenation of the Gibbs sampler, derived from the Gelman-Rubin statistic, which plays a key role in speeding up learning convergence. Our experiments show that our method, called Particle GFlowNets, markedly accelerates training in large combinatorial spaces.

</details>


### [267] [A Dataset and Model for Imputing Water Surface Elevation on a Large and Extremely Sparse Spatiotemporal Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11580)
*Ruben Cartuyvels,Karim Douch,Gabriele Bertoli,Mounia El Baz,Artemis Vrettou,Sébastien Lefèvre,Diego Fernandez Prieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AmazonSWE 是一个用于训练和评估大规模时空图插补方法的数据集，整合了来自多种来源的卫星测高数据（包括最新的宽幅SWOT传感器），覆盖亚马逊河流域19,000多条河段，时间跨度为2016-2026年。该数据集在稀疏性、拓扑结构和规模上均带来新挑战：每日观测不足1%的河段，且具有有向无环河流拓扑。现有插补方法不适用于此场景，因此本文提出一种双向选择性状态空间模型，通过采样连通子图并融合时空为单序列，结合拓扑感知位置编码，显著提升性能。相比现有最优方法（结合统计与物理建模），本模型在真实水位计验证下将RMSE降低18-39%，并实现对所有河段的预测，而非仅限于卫星覆盖充足的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于全球范围内实地水位计稀缺，难以建立可靠的水位建模框架；虽然卫星测高具备潜力，但其时间覆盖稀疏限制了应用。亟需一种能有效处理极稀疏、大规模、具有复杂河流拓扑结构的时空数据插补方法，以支持洪水预警、水资源管理和全球水循环研究。

Method: 提出一种双向选择性状态空间模型，通过采样连通子图，将时空维度扁平化为单一令牌序列，并引入拓扑感知的位置编码，以适应河流网络的有向无环结构和极端稀疏性。

Result: 所提模型在亚马逊河流域数据集上显著优于现有方法，相比当前最优的SWOT水位密度化方法（融合统计与物理建模），在真实水位计验证下将RMSE降低18-39%；同时实现对全部河段的预测，而非仅限于卫星覆盖密集区域。

Conclusion: AmazonSWE 数据集为河流系统水位连续监测提供了新的基准，所提出的模型有效应对了极端稀疏性与复杂拓扑带来的挑战，展现出在实际应用中的巨大潜力，可推动全球尺度水文建模的发展。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring of water surface elevation across river networks is critical for flood forecasting, water resource management, and understanding the global water cycle. Yet, the scarcity of in situ gauges across much of the globe constrains the development of reliable modeling frameworks. Satellite altimetry has the potential to alleviate this problem but its use is currently hindered by sparse temporal coverage. To this end, we introduce AmazonSWE, a dataset for training and evaluating large-scale spatiotemporal graph imputation methods that integrates processed satellite altimetry measurements from a range of sources, including the recent wide-swath SWOT sensor. The dataset covers over 19K river sections and 10 years (2016-2026) in the Amazon river basin, with in situ gauges held out for evaluation. Besides contributing a novel real-world use case with the potential for societal impact, AmazonSWE introduces significant technical challenges: with fewer than 1% of sections observed per day, the dataset is far sparser than existing imputation benchmarks, and its directed acyclic river topology is both structurally different from and larger than graphs in existing datasets. We show that prior spatiotemporal graph imputation methods are not adapted to this topology, scale and sparsity, and propose a simple bidirectional selective state space model that outperforms them by sampling connected subgraphs and flattening space and time into a single token sequence with topology-aware positional encodings. Compared to the state-of-the-art published method for SWOT-based WSE densification, which integrates statistics with physical modeling, our model reduces RMSE against in situ gauges by 18-39%, while producing predictions for every river section rather than only those with sufficient nearby satellite coverage.

</details>


### [268] [LoaDiff: Conditional Generation of Electricity Consumption Time Series for Energy Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11639)
*Mariia Baranova,Adrien Petralia,Etienne Le Naour,Nathan Etourneau,Guillaume Hofmann,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoaDiff是一种基于扩散模型的生成方法，用于创建真实且多样化的家庭级电力负荷曲线，支持静态（如家电拥有情况）和动态（如日历信息、室外温度）条件控制，在负荷预测和电器识别等下游任务中表现良好，同时避免过度记忆训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于隐私和数据保护法规限制，获取真实细粒度智能电表数据困难，因此需要生成逼真的合成数据以支持能源研究与应用。

Method: 提出一种基于扩散模型的生成框架LoaDiff，能够生成年尺度、亚小时级的家庭用电负荷曲线，并支持多种静态和动态条件变量的灵活调节。

Result: LoaDiff在真实性、多样性、下游任务适用性以及对条件变化的响应能力方面均优于多个基线模型；未发现明显训练数据记忆现象，具备良好的实用性和可控性。

Conclusion: LoaDiff为住宅用电行为研究提供了高质量的合成数据解决方案，适用于负载预测、电器检测及需求侧灵活性分析等关键能源应用场景。

Abstract: The energy transition is reshaping residential electricity consumption through the increasing adoption of distributed generation, electrified appliances, and demand-response programs. Understanding these evolving behaviors requires access to granular smart-meter data for applications such as load forecasting, appliance detection, and demand-side flexibility analysis. However, such data are subject to strict access restrictions and data-protection regulations. Thus, realistic synthetic alternatives are necessary. In this paper, we introduce LoaDiff, a diffusion-based generative model for year-long, sub-hourly smart-meter load curves. LoaDiff supports flexible conditioning on static household attributes, such as appliance ownership, and dynamic contextual variables, including calendar information and outdoor temperature. We evaluate the model against multiple generative baselines on three residential electricity-consumption datasets. Our experiments assess four complementary dimensions: fidelity and diversity, training-record memorization risk, downstream utility for load forecasting and appliance detection, and conditional controllability under alternative temperature conditions. The results show that LoaDiff generates realistic and diverse load profiles, achieves a favorable trade-off between generation quality and limited evidence of memorization, preserves information useful for downstream energy applications, and responds coherently to changes in conditioning variables.

</details>


### [269] [RDDMPI: Residual Denoising Diffusion Model for Probabilistic Multivariate Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11648)
*Ramiro Valdes Jara,David Chapman,Adam Meyers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RDDMPI提出一种在残差空间中进行条件残差扩散的框架，通过将完整信号分解为预训练模型的基线预测和残差不确定性，简化了扩散学习目标，从而提升多变量时间序列插补的精度与不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的方法直接在原始数据空间中进行去噪，需同时捕捉全局结构、时序动态和随机变化，任务复杂；而现代确定性插补器已能提供准确初始重建，因此可利用其作为基线，专注于建模残差不确定性以降低生成难度。

Method: RDDMPI采用基线-残差分解策略，将缺失信号建模为预训练模型输出的基线信号与扩散过程生成的残差之和；在反向去噪过程中，条件化于基线完成信号及其潜在表示，并引入可靠性感知机制自适应控制基线信息的影响，使扩散模型专注修正结构化误差而非重建全信号。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，RDDMPI在重构精度和不确定性量化方面均优于现有方法，表现出更强的稳定性和性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过在残差空间中进行条件扩散建模，RDDMPI有效降低了生成任务复杂度，显著提升了多变量时间序列插补的质量与可信度，为高维时序数据修复提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multivariate time series imputation (MTSI) aims to recover missing values in temporal data composed of multiple interdependent variables. This problem is central to real-world applications such as healthcare monitoring, traffic networks, and energy systems. Recent diffusion-based approaches have shown strong potential for probabilistic imputation by learning to generate missing values through iterative denoising. However, most existing approaches perform diffusion directly in the original data space, requiring the denoising network to simultaneously capture global structure, temporal dynamics, and stochastic variability. This makes the generative task unnecessarily complex, especially when modern deterministic imputers can already provide accurate initial reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose RDDMPI, a conditional residual diffusion framework that operates directly in residual space. Instead of modeling the full missing signal directly, we reformulate probabilistic imputation as a baseline-residual decomposition, where a pretrained model captures the dominant signal and a diffusion process models the residual uncertainty. To better exploit deterministic guidance, \model{} conditions the reverse denoising process on both the baseline-completed signal and its latent representation, while a reliability-aware conditioning mechanism adaptively controls the influence of baseline information during residual generation. This formulation simplifies the diffusion learning objective, enabling it to focus on structured correction terms rather than reconstructing the full signal. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that RDDMPI consistently improves both reconstruction accuracy and uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [270] [Musec: MomentUm SpEctral Clipping for Stable Muon-type Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11655)
*Zhuanghua Liu,Menglian Wang,Luo Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Musec（MomentUm SpEctral Clipping），一种针对Muon优化器训练不稳定性问题的改进方法。通过用谱裁剪替代原有的谱平坦化，保留动量矩阵的原始谱结构，同时限制过大的奇异值，实现无需架构修改的稳定训练。进一步提出Soft Musec，采用光滑的谱饱和函数与耦合牛顿-舒尔兹迭代高效实现。理论证明了Musec在非凸非光滑随机优化中的收敛性，为首个此类保证。实验表明Soft Musec在多种学习率和模型规模下显著提升稳定性，且在发散场景中仍保持稳定，同时在调优配置下性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器虽在大语言模型训练中表现优异，但存在因谱平坦化导致的训练不稳定问题，如损失突增和权重无界增长。现有解决方案依赖于特定架构的权重或注意力对数裁剪，缺乏通用性且未从根本上解决各组件的不稳定性。因此需要一种通用、优化器级别的稳定机制。

Method: 提出Musec，将Muon的谱平坦化替换为谱裁剪：仅对超过阈值的奇异值进行裁剪，保留动量矩阵的原始谱结构。进一步设计Soft Musec，利用光滑的谱饱和函数结合耦合牛顿-舒尔兹迭代实现高效计算。

Result: Soft Musec在不同学习率和模型规模下均显著提升训练稳定性，可在其他Muon变体发散的场景中保持稳定；在参数调优情况下性能与原有方法相当，且首次为Muon类方法提供了非凸非光滑环境下的收敛性理论保障。

Conclusion: Musec及其高效版本Soft Musec提供了一种通用、架构无关的优化器级训练稳定性解决方案，有效克服了Muon的固有缺陷，兼具理论严谨性与实践有效性。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a highly effective optimizer for large language model training, often achieving superior convergence and performance compared with the widely adopted Adam and AdamW optimizers. Nevertheless, Muon is prone to training instability due to its spectral flattening, manifested by loss spikes and unbounded growth of model weights. Existing approaches primarily rely on weight or attention-logit clipping, which require architecture-specific modifications and do not directly address instability across all model components. We propose MomentUm SpEctral Clipping (Musec), which replaces Muon's spectral flattening with spectral clipping: rather than setting all singular values of the momentum matrix to approximately one, Musec clips singular values that exceed a threshold while preserving the underlying spectral structure of the momentum. Our strategy provides an optimizer-level, architecture-agnostic mechanism for stabilizing Muon training. We further develop Soft Musec, an efficient implementation that uses a smooth spectral saturation function approximated by coupled Newton-Schulz iterations. Theoretically, we establish convergence guarantees for Musec in nonconvex nonsmooth stochastic optimization. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first convergence guarantee for Muon-type methods in the nonconvex nonsmooth setting. We provide empirical studies to show that Soft Musec consistently improves training stability over existing Muon variants across a wide range of learning rates and model sizes. Notably, Soft Musec remains stable in settings where existing Muon variants diverge, while matching their performance under well-tuned configurations.

</details>


### [271] [Learnware and AI Model Management System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11656)
*Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从AI模型存储向AI模型管理系统的转型，核心是引入'learnware'（模型+规范）概念，通过不访问训练数据生成可比较的规范，实现模型的识别、复用与组合。所提出的Learnware Dock System (LDS) 为异构模型协作提供协议基础，支持跨开发者、跨任务的智能系统协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型池仅作为存储系统，缺乏对不同开发者、任务、数据和目标下模型的有效管理与复用能力。由于训练数据不可共享，传统方法难以在不接触原始数据的前提下理解或组合模型功能，亟需一种新型管理机制。

Method: 将基本管理单元从模型升级为learnware（模型+规范），利用机器学习生成规范，该规范在不泄露训练数据的前提下具备数据保全性质，并基于公开参考标准实现跨模型可比性。通过Learnware Dock System (LDS) 实现模型的发现、评估与组装。

Result: 成功构建了无需访问训练数据或用户原始数据即可识别、复用和组合异构模型的管理框架；规范可作为通用协作协议，支持独立开发的智能体间协同工作。

Conclusion: Learnware及其管理机制为迈向真正的AI模型管理系统提供了可行路径，推动了跨开发者、跨任务的AI能力集成与协作，是实现下一代AI基础设施的关键一步。

Abstract: The transition from file storage to database management systems transformed stored data into managed resources. AI now faces an analogous transition from AI model storage to AI model management. Existing model pools essentially serve as \textit{AI model storage systems}. What is needed instead are \textit{AI model management systems} that enable models trained by different developers, for different tasks, with different data, and under different objectives to be identified, reused, and even assembled to address future user tasks. Because AI model developers are generally unwilling to share their training data, such systems should operate without accessing the training data of model developers and, ideally, without accessing raw data of future users. This requirement poses a fundamental challenge: the functionality of a modern AI model may not be fully understood even by the developer who trained it. How, then, can a system identify which models are useful for a given user task, let alone assemble models developed independently for different purposes? At first glance, this objective may appear unattainable. It becomes possible, however, by upgrading the basic unit of management from a machine learning model to a \textit{learnware}. \textit{Learnware = Model + Specification}. The specification, whose assignment transforms a trained model into a learnware, is generated with the help of a machine learning process without disclosing the training data of the developer and has a theoretically established data-preservation property. The \textit{Learnware Dock System (LDS)} provides a path toward powerful AI model management systems. Because specifications are generated according to a published reference and are comparable across models, they can also serve as an AI model \textit{collaboration protocol} through which independently developed models, including intelligent agents, can collaborate.

</details>


### [272] [Why Does Post-Training Quantization Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11716)
*Yuxiang Chen,Michael Beyer,Jun Zhu,Jianfei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了后训练量化为何能有效压缩大语言模型，尽管量化引入了误差。研究发现，预训练模型在量化后仍能保持良好性能，主要归因于两个机制：一是每层引入的量化误差倾向于抵消前一层继承的误差，从而缓慢积累；二是语言模型输出头的几何结构优先保留高排名词的概率，使关键预测不受影响。这两个机制在预训练过程中形成，解释了量化误差虽经多层传播但对输出影响甚微的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究后训练量化为何能在不经过量化训练的情况下仍保持模型性能，理解其背后的鲁棒性机制。

Method: 通过对比全精度与量化前向传播，分析误差累积行为，量化评估误差抵消效应，并研究语言模型输出头的几何特性对高排名词预测的影响。

Result: 发现了误差部分抵消和输出头对高排名词概率的保护作用，二者共同解释了量化误差在深层网络中缓慢增长且对下游任务影响小的现象。实验验证了这些发现适用于多种模型和量化设置。

Conclusion: 后训练量化之所以有效，是因为预训练过程中形成的误差抵消机制和输出头对高置信度预测的保护作用，使得量化误差即使在深度网络中传播也难以显著破坏模型性能。

Abstract: Post-training quantization compresses large language models (LLMs) by storing their weights at reduced precision, and each quantized weight introduces an error into the hidden states. Naively, these errors should accumulate with depth and corrupt next-token prediction; randomly initialized models accumulate these discrepancies rapidly, whereas quantized pretrained models accumulate much less hidden-state error and largely maintain downstream task performance, even though they were never trained with quantization noise. This raises the question we address: why does post-training quantization work? Comparing full-precision and quantized forward passes, we identify two mechanisms that characterize pretrained quantization robustness. First, the error a layer newly introduces tends to oppose the error it inherits from the layer's input. The two cancel partially such that the discrepancy between full-precision and quantized passes grows slowly. This counteracting residual interaction develops during pretraining. Our quantitative analysis identifies it as a major factor slowing hidden-error growth. Second, LM-head geometry preferentially preserves the scores and probabilities of high-ranked tokens, which typically represent the model's most confident predictions. Together, these mechanisms explain why quantization error that passes through numerous layers can still produce only small output changes, and we verify the findings across models and quantization settings.

</details>


### [273] [Predicting Privacy Leakage from Weight Spectral Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11780)
*Richard J. Preen,Jim Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于谱特征的轻量级指标是否可作为隐私泄露风险（MIA）的代理指标。实验表明，稳定秩与整体MIA成功率呈强正相关，而Log alpha-Norm在低误报率下与MIA脆弱性呈一致负相关，且这些关联比传统泛化差距更强，说明神经网络谱信息可能包含未被传统过拟合度量捕捉的隐私泄露信号，支持谱分析作为可扩展隐私审计的新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的成员推断攻击（MIA）需要训练计算成本高昂的影子模型，难以实现大规模隐私评估。因此亟需一种低成本、高效的隐私泄露评估方法。

Method: 采用WeightWatcher框架下的谱指标（如稳定秩、Log alpha-Norm），在图像和表格分类任务上评估其与MIA隐私泄露的相关性，并与传统泛化度量进行对比。

Result: 稳定秩与MIA成功率呈强正相关，Log alpha-Norm在低误报率下与MIA脆弱性呈负相关，且相关性优于一般化差距。

Conclusion: 神经网络的谱结构中蕴含了关于隐私泄露的信息，该信息无法被传统过拟合度量完全捕获，表明谱分析是实现高效、可扩展隐私审计的有前景方向。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are widely used to audit the privacy disclosure risk of machine learning models, however current state-of-the-art attacks require training computationally expensive shadow models, making large-scale privacy evaluation impractical. In this work, we investigate whether inexpensive spectral metrics derived from the heavy-tailed self-regularisation framework can serve as proxies for MIA vulnerability. We evaluate several WeightWatcher spectral metrics on image and tabular classification tasks and compare their relationship with MIA privacy leakage against conventional measures of generalisation. Across datasets, stable rank exhibits a strong positive correlation with overall MIA success, while Log alpha-Norm shows a consistent negative correlation with MIA vulnerability at the low false-positive regime. These associations are observed to be stronger than those obtained using the generalisation gap. The results indicate that neural network spectra may contain information about privacy leakage that is not fully captured by conventional measures of overfitting, motivating spectral analysis as a promising direction for scalable privacy auditing.

</details>


### [274] [Dynamic language model representations for multi-objective reaction optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11790)
*Joshua W. Sin,David Ming Segura,Bojana Ranković,Siu Lun Chau,Marius D. R. Lutz,Andrea Anelli,Ryan P. Burwood,Kurt Püntener,Maximilian J. Notheis,Raphael Bigler,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于文本的动态反应表示学习方法，通过微调语言模型与高斯过程代理模型联合训练，实现多目标贝叶斯优化中的任务自适应表示。该方法在镍和钯催化交叉偶联反应中，以更少实验次数达到优化收敛，且在氰化反应和不对称氢化反应中实现了高产率和高对映选择性，验证了其高效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统化学反应优化依赖于固定的特征表示，如独热编码或分子描述符，但这些方法在结构和功能多样性的反应组件上表现不佳，且难以跨系统迁移。构建共享表示本身是复杂且重复的工作，亟需一种无需预先定义特征的通用解决方案。

Method: 利用微调的语言模型对反应条件的文本描述进行编码，结合高斯过程代理模型，在多目标贝叶斯优化框架中动态学习反应表示，实现任务自适应的优化策略。

Result: 在多种催化反应中，该方法以更少实验次数实现优化收敛；在前瞻性应用中，192次高通量实验（不足设计空间的3%）即获得高产率（94%、84%）和高对映选择性（99.6% ee），并成功放大至克级规模。

Conclusion: 通过从文本中动态学习反应表示，该方法克服了传统特征表示的局限性，显著提升多目标反应优化的效率与可扩展性，为自动化化学合成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Optimising chemical reactions across multiple objectives, such as yield, selectivity, and safety, is central to chemical synthesis, and model-driven approaches depend critically on how reaction components are represented. Established featurisations are either chemically uninformative, as with one-hot encodings, or, as with molecular descriptors, do not readily extend across chemically distinct components. For structurally and functionally diverse components, it is therefore unclear what a shared representation should contain. Constructing such a representation is itself a challenging research undertaking that must be revisited for each new reaction system. Here we bypass this step by learning the reaction representation dynamically from text. Textual descriptions of reaction conditions are encoded by a fine-tuned language model trained jointly with Gaussian process surrogates, yielding task-adaptive representations within a multi-objective Bayesian optimisation loop. Across nickel- and palladium-catalysed cross-couplings in both sequential and parallel experimentation regimes, this approach reaches optimisation convergence in fewer experiments than descriptor libraries or one-hot encoding. Applied prospectively to a palladium-catalysed cyanation spanning mixed ligand denticity and heterogeneous additives, and to a three-objective asymmetric hydrogenation across chiral iridium and ruthenium catalyst families, two rounds of high-throughput experimentation (192 reactions, under 3% of each design space) delivered conditions translating directly to gram scale in 94% and 84% isolated yield, the latter at 99.6% enantiomeric excess.

</details>


### [275] [Thinking with Looped Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11801)
*Ayhan Suleymanzade,Chanhyuk Lee,Floor Eijkelboom,Nicholas M. Boffi,İsmail İlkan Ceylan,Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Looped flows propose a novel training method for looped models in deep learning that uses local denoising objectives with progressively decreasing noise levels and shared noise to enable effective recurrence learning. During inference, the model integrates probability flow velocities parameterized by the learned denoiser, allowing more computation through finer temporal grids and supporting multiple valid predictions. This approach improves performance on reasoning benchmarks, achieving 58.8% accuracy on ARC-AGI-1 and 12.2% on ARC-AGI-2.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing looped models struggle during training because backpropagation only covers a few recurrence steps, making it difficult to train early updates to support later ones. The motivation is to develop a method that enables effective transfer of computation across recurrence steps despite limited gradient exposure.

Method: Looped flows use local denoising objectives with temporally structured noise (progressively decreasing levels and shared noise) to train recurrence. Inference is formulated as integrating a probability flow velocity field derived from the denoiser, enabling fine-grained computation over time and multiple predictions from different initial noise samples.

Result: The proposed method outperforms prior state-of-the-art looped models on six reasoning benchmarks, including two multi-solution tasks, achieving 58.8% test accuracy on ARC-AGI-1 and 12.2% on ARC-AGI-2.

Conclusion: Looped flows effectively address the training instability in recurrent inference models by leveraging structured denoising objectives and probability flow integration, enabling better scalability of computation and improved performance on complex reasoning tasks.

Abstract: Humans and machines often solve harder problems by spending more time on computation. In deep learning, looped models implement this idea during inference by recurrently updating a hidden state. In practice, however, their training backpropagates through only one or a few updates, making it hard to train early updates to support future ones. We propose looped flows, an approach that sidesteps this issue by training the recurrence with local denoising objectives. By imposing temporal association across denoising objectives through progressively decreasing noise levels and shared noise, the model is incentivized to learn recurrent states that transfer useful computation over time, even when gradients cover only a few updates. We then formulate inference as integrating the velocity of a probability flow parameterized by the learned denoiser, coupled with recurrent states. This allows solving harder problems by spending more computation through a finer temporal grid and enables multiple valid predictions from different initial noise samples. Across six reasoning benchmarks including two multi-solution benchmarks, looped flows outperform prior state-of-the-art looped models overall, achieving 58.8% test accuracy on ARC-AGI-1 and 12.2% on ARC-AGI-2.

</details>


### [276] [Model-Aware Schedules Improve Generation via Fiberwise Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11842)
*Luyi Jia,Boyan Zhang,Yilun Liu,Steffen Rulands*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于纤维最优传输的模型感知调度构造方法，通过在概率路径上定义纤维内预测风险并结合系数路径动能作用，实现闭式最优时间分配。该方法在多种模型、数据集和训练配置下均优于强基线，尤其在流匹配上实现CIFAR-10上38.6%相对FID降低。不同模型的归一化风险分布高度一致，表明其具有经验普适性，并可借助预训练检查点进行风险估计。冻结的解析分配模板也能保留大部分性能提升，无需额外风险估计或模型特定拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最优传输的扩散与流匹配调度方法虽能解释强基线表现，但为模型无关且忽略预测误差。本文旨在引入模型感知的调度构造，以更准确地反映实际预测性能并提升生成质量。

Method: 提出基于纤维最优传输的模型感知调度构造：在固定时间和状态的路径上，定义信号/噪声分解的仿射纤维；通过平均真实与预测分解间的最优传输成本构建纤维内预测风险；结合该风险与系数路径动能作用，导出闭式最优时间分配；支持一般线性预测目标，风险可从早期检查点估计。

Result: 在DDPM与流匹配等多种设置下，模型感知调度显著优于模型无关基线，尤其在流匹配于CIFAR-10上达到38.6%相对FID下降（仅16次函数评估）。不同模型的归一化风险分布对齐良好，表明存在经验普适性；冻结解析模板亦能保留大部分性能优势。

Conclusion: 所提模型感知调度有效整合了预测误差与最优传输原理，实现高性能且具普适性的生成调度策略，其核心思想可通过通用模板复用，具备广泛适用潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching schedules control the signal and noise coefficients that mix data and noise along affine probability paths. Minimizing a kinetic action defined on coefficient paths, motivated by optimal transport, helps explain strong baselines but remains model-agnostic and ignores prediction error. Here we introduce a model-aware schedule construction based on fiberwise optimal transport. At a fixed time and state on the probability path, compatible signal/noise decompositions form an affine fiber. We define a fiberwise prediction risk by averaging optimal-transport costs between the true and predictor-induced decompositions within these fibers. On a fixed coefficient curve, combining this risk with coefficient-path kinetic action yields a closed-form optimal time allocation. This construction extends to general linear prediction targets, and the risk profile can be estimated from an early baseline checkpoint. We evaluate DDPMs and flow matching across prediction targets, training configurations, risk-estimation checkpoints, datasets, and architectures. Our model-aware schedules consistently outperform strong baselines, including a 38.6% relative FID reduction for flow matching on CIFAR-10 at 16 function evaluations. Each model-agnostic kinetic baseline determines its own kinetic reference coordinate. In these coordinates, fiberwise-risk profiles from independently trained models in different settings align closely after normalization to unit area. The resulting schedule deformations used in training also align, suggesting empirical universality across the evaluated models and settings. Pretrained-checkpoint diagnostics extend this normalized-risk agreement to larger conditional latent diffusion and 2-RF models. A frozen analytic allocation template retains most of the model-aware improvement without further risk estimation or model-specific fitting.

</details>


### [277] [AdamX: Cosine similarity meets gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11867)
*Francisco Caldas,Ruben Belo,Cláudia Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdamX is a scalable, model-agnostic first-order optimizer that uses cosine similarity to adaptively control update magnitudes and includes a variance rectification scheme for smoother early training. It achieves competitive convergence rates across various benchmarks with a fixed hyperparameter budget.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To improve optimization stability and convergence speed in deep learning training by introducing adaptive update control via cosine similarity and variance rectification, while maintaining ease of integration into existing pipelines.

Method: AdamX employs cosine similarity to adaptively scale parameter updates and incorporates a variance rectification mechanism to stabilize early-stage training. It is designed as a plug-and-play alternative to existing optimizers like Adam.

Result: Empirical results show that AdamX reaches predefined performance thresholds faster than baseline optimizers on multiple datasets and architectures, demonstrating competitive convergence rates under a fixed hyperparameter budget.

Conclusion: AdamX is an effective, scalable, and easy-to-integrate optimizer that enhances training stability and convergence speed, making it a promising alternative for diverse deep learning applications.

Abstract: We introduce AdamX, a first-order optimizer that incorporates cosine similarity as an adaptive mechanism for controlling update magnitudes. The proposed method is scalable, model-agnostic, and straightforward to integrate into existing training pipelines. We further introduce a variance rectification scheme that promotes smoother optimization during the early stages of training. Overall, we provide empirical evidence that AdamX achieves competitive convergence rates across a range of benchmark datasets and architectures. Performance is evaluated in terms of the number of epochs required to reach predefined performance thresholds under a fixed hyperparameter budget. Code and Experiments available at: https://github.com/FranciscoCaldas/adamX.

</details>


### [278] [The Last AI Built by Humans: Toward Genuine Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11873)
*Yi Duan,Ying Liu,Zirui Tang,Haodong Chen,Jun Zhou,Yumou Liu,Bangrui Xu,Yukai Wu,Sidi Chen,Yuhan Zhou,Haoyu Wang,Xiaoyou Yu,Shaokun Han,Xuzhou Zhu,Le Zhou,Bolin Lu,Wei Zhou,Jiachen Liu,Nuozhou Fang,Jiaxin Tian,Ruoyu Chen,Yuxuan Li,Kai Zuo,Kaiyan Zhang,Jiantao Qiu,Conghui He,Guoliang Li,Bowen Zhou,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhoufutu Wen,Jihua Kang,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了递归自我改进（RSI）在AI系统中的应用，通过头盔闭合指数（HCI）揭示现有大语言模型的问题，提出从执行自主、策略自主、经验获取自主到环境适应自主，最终实现递归元改进的发展路线。文章分析了科学发现、具身智能、软件工程等场景下的RSI需求与进展速度差异，并结合行业实践和初步实证数据，指出实现真正RSI的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型存在局限性，难以持续自我优化；为提升AI系统的长期进化能力，需引入递归自我改进机制。

Method: 采用头盔闭合指数（HCI）评估当前模型瓶颈，构建递归自我改进的五阶段发展路径，跨场景对比分析其适用性，并结合行业实践与实证研究识别关键障碍。

Result: 揭示了现有大模型在自我改进方面的不足，提出了系统化的RSI发展框架，明确了不同应用场景下的差异化需求与技术挑战。

Conclusion: 实现真正的递归自我改进仍面临重大挑战，但通过分阶段推进与跨领域协同，有望推动AI系统走向更深层次的自主进化。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) enables AI systems to turn experience and feedback into persistent changes that improve both their capabilities and the process of future improvement. We first use the Headroom-Closed Index (HCI) to reveal the problems of existing LLMs, then introduce the RSI concept and its development roadmap: from improvement-execution autonomy, improvement-strategy autonomy, experience-acquisition autonomy, and environment-adaptation autonomy, to recursive meta-improvement. Next we examine RSI across scenarios (e.g., scientific discovery, embodied intelligence, software engineering), highlighting their distinct requirements and development speeds. Drawing on diverse industry practices and preliminary empirical evidence, we connect RSI research with practical systems and identify key challenges to achieving genuine RSI.

</details>


### [279] [CoRA-NAS: Coarse Ranking and Anchor-Residual Refinement for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11884)
*Yifan Yang,Zhaoyan Wang,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoRA-NAS提出一种两阶段框架，结合静态排名先验与低成本学习曲线精炼，通过log-rank共识和无目标共识门实现初始排名，再利用锚点样本的早期验证曲线与ExtraTrees模型传播残差修正，仅需约1%的训练成本。该方法无需全量训练标签，跨搜索空间统一配置，仅调整架构编码。在NAS-Bench-201、101、TransNAS-Bench-101和NATS-SSS上分别达到0.946、0.715、0.786、0.894的平均斯皮尔曼相关性，其中最低相关性优于现有方法；在CIFAR-100上选中架构达73.32%准确率，接近最优73.37%。在纯大小空间中，精炼弥补了静态先验的不足，同时保持与最强容量代理相当性能。整体兼具跨空间鲁棒性与低开销架构选择能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零成本代理在不同搜索空间中可靠性不一，亟需一种跨空间稳定且低成本的高效架构排名方法。

Method: 提出CoRA-NAS两阶段框架：第一阶段CoRA-Rank通过等权log-rank共识与目标无关共识门整合容量与结构初始化代理；第二阶段CoRA-Refine采样锚点，外推其早期验证曲线，并用ExtraTrees模型传播学习到的残差修正，仅消耗约1%的训练成本。

Result: 在NAS-Bench-201、101、TransNAS-Bench-101和NATS-SSS上平均斯皮尔曼相关性分别为0.946、0.715、0.786、0.894，最低相关性高于对比方法；在NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100上选中架构达73.32%准确率，接近最优73.37%；在纯大小空间中有效弥补静态先验缺陷，性能与最强容量代理相当。

Conclusion: CoRA-NAS实现了跨搜索空间的稳健排名与极低成本的架构选择，兼具高相关性与通用性，是当前零成本架构评估的有效范式。

Abstract: Zero-cost proxies rank architectures cheaply, but their reliability varies across search spaces. We introduce CoRA-NAS (COarse Ranking + Anchor-residual), a two-stage framework combining a static ranking prior with low-cost learning-curve refinement. CoRA-Rank aggregates capacity and structure-at-initialization proxies through an equal-weight log-rank consensus and a target-free consensus gate. CoRA-Refine samples anchors across this prior, extrapolates their early validation curves, and propagates a learned residual correction with an ExtraTrees model. The refinement uses approximately 1% of the cost of fully training the candidate set. Fully trained architecture-accuracy labels are not used to fit the ranker. One configuration is used across spaces, with space-specific architecture encodings. Across NAS-Bench-201, NAS-Bench-101, TransNAS-Bench-101, and NATS-SSS, CoRA-Refine achieves mean Spearman correlations of 0.946, 0.715, 0.786, and 0.894, respectively. Its worst-space correlation of 0.715 is the highest among the compared methods. On NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100, its selected architecture reaches 73.32% accuracy, near the reported ground-truth best of 73.37%. On the pure size space, refinement recovers the static prior's shortfall relative to parameter count, while remaining tied with the strongest capacity proxies within noise. The resulting framework combines cross-space ranking robustness with low-cost architecture selection.

</details>


### [280] [CausalArena: Benchmarking Causal Discovery in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11897)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalArena，一个统一且可演化的因果发现基准，涵盖合成SCM、语义操作SCM和公式基础SCM，以应对现有评估方法在图结构、机制和协议上的不一致问题。实验表明不同方法在各类基准上的表现差异显著，强调了基准多样性与预训练-评估重叠是基础模型时代因果发现评估的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现评估依赖于结构因果模型（SCM），但不同研究在图家族、生成机制和评估协议上差异大；同时，因果发现基础模型（CDFMs）的出现使性能可能受预训练环境与测试数据重叠的影响，导致固定合成基准的结果难以解释。

Method: 提出CausalArena基准，包含三类SCM：合成SCM（控制结构与机制多样性）、语义操作SCM（提供人类可读的语义环境）、公式基础SCM（基于显式科学机制）。结合真实世界公开数据集进行外部有效性验证。

Result: 在经典、神经网络及预训练方法上进行实验，发现不同方法在各类SCM和评估协议下的排名存在显著变化，表明单一基准上的优异表现不能可靠转移到其他场景。

Conclusion: 基准多样性与预训练-评估重叠是当前因果发现评估中的核心挑战，需建立更统一、多维度、可演化的评估框架。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover causal structures from data and is fundamental to scientific reasoning and intervention-based decision making. Its evaluation relies heavily on structural causal models (SCMs), which specify a causal graph together with the mechanisms that generate data, yet existing studies differ substantially in graph families, mechanisms, and evaluation protocols.
